1b1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//bam po

1b3(phal chen): sbyor ba mtha' yas pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la mnyam par bzhag go/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' 
1b4(phal chen): chos kyi blo gros kyis/byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sbyor ba mtha' yas pa zhes bde la mnyam par 
1b5(phal chen): bzhag ma thag tu/de'i mod la phyogs bcu'i phyogs re re'i 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi

2a1(phal chen): @##//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis kyang 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//chos kyi blo gros khyod de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin sbyor ba mtha' yas pa la mnyam par bzhag pa legs so 
2a2(phal chen): legs so//de bas na rigs kyi bu khyod la phyogs bcu nas de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang mtshan ma thun pa sha dag gis bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par 
2a3(phal chen): snang mdzad kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang /khyod bdag gi dge ba'i rtsa bas nye bar bstan pa'i 'di lta ste//chos thams cad bstan par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings 
2a4(phal chen): su chud par bya ba dang /sems can gyi khams rnam par bsgom pa dang /chags pa med pa nyid du chud par bya ba dang /bsgribs pa med pa'i spyod yul thob par bya ba dang /kun nas sbyor ba thob par bya ba dang /thams 
2a5(phal chen): cad mkhyen pa nyid yongs su gzung bar bya ba dang /chos thams cad khong du chud par bya ba dang /dbang po thams cad yongs su shes par bya ba dang /chos bstan pa thams cad yongs su gzung ba dang /'di lta ste/byang chub 
2a6(phal chen): sems dpa'i rnam par bgod pa bcu la rtsom par bya ba'i phyir byin gyis brlabs pa mdzad kyis/rigs kyi bu khyod kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin brlabs kyis chos bstan pa 'di la spobs pa bskyed cig/
2a7(phal chen): de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros la chags pa med pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do//thogs pa med pa nye bar bsgru bar mdzad do//mchod pa med pa nye bar bsgrub
2a8(phal chen): pa mdzad do//rmongs pa med par nye bar bsgrub par mdzad do//tha dad pa med pa nye bar bsgrubs par mdzad do//kha na ma thob med pa nye bar bsgrub par mdzad de//dpag tu med pa nye bar bsgrub pa'i mdzad do//
2b1(phal chen): zhum pa med pa nye bar bsgrub par mdzad do//'phrogs pa med pa nye bar bsgrubs par mdzad do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/ting nge 'dzin 'di nyid kyi chos nyid thob pa'i phyir ro//de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
2b2(phal chen): de dag gis/byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros kyi spyi bo la phyag g.yas pa byug pa mdzad do//de ltar sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da' de dag gis reg ma thag du byang chub sems dpa' chosakyi blo gros ting nge 'dzin 
2b3(phal chen): de las langs te/byang chub sems dpa' de dag la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/byang chub sems dpa'i rigs shes bya ba 'di ni chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/nam mkha'i mthas gtugs pas 
2b4(phal chen): shin tu yangs pa ste/de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni 'das pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'ad rnams ni rigs su skye ba yin//ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das rnams kyi rigs su skye ba yin//
2b5(phal chen): da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan das rnams kyi rigs su skyes ba yin//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chu sems dpa' brtson par byed pa gang las bsams te/de skad du smras pa de dag gang zhe na/kye rgyal 
2b6(phal chen): ba'i sras dag bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rna ma par dgod pa ste/'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis gsungs pa dang /gsung ba dang /
2b7(phal chen): gsung bar 'gyur ba dag go/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sems dang po skyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dgod pa dang /las dang po pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dgod pa dang rnal 
2b8(phal chen): 'byor la spyod pa'i byang chub sems dang rnam par dgod pa dang cher skyes pa'i byang chub sems dpal rnam par dgod pa dang sbyor ba phun sum 'tshogs pa'i byang chub sems dba' rnam par dgod pa dang
3a1(phal chen): @#//bsam pa phun sum tshogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dgod pa dang /phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dgod pa dang /gzhon nur gyur pa'i byang chub sems dpal rnam par 
3a2(phal chen): dgod pa dang /rgyal mtshan gyi byang chub sems dpa' rnams par dgod pa dang /dbang skur ba'i byang chub sems dpa' rna ma par dgod pa dang /kye rgyal ba'i sras dag bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnamapar dgod 
3a3(phal chen): pa ste|'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis gsungs pa dang /gsung pa dang /gsung par 'gyur ba dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la sems dang po bskyed pa'i 
3a4(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dga' 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams mthong nas/mdzes pa dang bzang ba dang /mig du 'ong ba dang /kha dog rgyas pa dang /thub par 
3a5(phal chen): dka' ba dang /mthu che bar mthong ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pa dang /yongs su bstan pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang /rjes su bstan pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa mthong ba'am/sems can sdug bsngal mthong ba'am/
3a6(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos rgya chen po thos par gyur nas/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du smon cing snga phyi med par byang chub tu sems bskyed de/sems 
3a7(phal chen): bskyed pa dang po des ni thub par dka' ba'i gnas bcu yongs su bzung bar 'gyur te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar gnas dang gnas ma yin pa'i shes pa dang /las kyi rnam par smin par shes pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /
3a8(phal chen): rnam par byang ba shes pa dang /dbang po dang brtson 'grus rnam pa tha dad pa yongs su rdzogs pa mchog dang tha ma shes pa dang /mos pa tha dad pa dang /mos pa du ma shes pa dang /khams tha dad pa dang khams du ma shes pa dang /
3b1(phal chen): thams cad du 'gro ba'i lam shes pa dang /bsam gtan dang /rnam par thar pa dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms par 'jug pa dang /kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang ba dang /rjes su bsgribs pa 
3b2(phal chen): med pa shes pa dang /chags pa med pa'i lha'i mig shes pa dang /dus gsum snyam pa nyid du zag pa med pa shes pa'o//thub par bka' ba'i gnas bcu po de dag yongs su zin par 'gyur te/de ni sems dang po bskyed 
3b3(phal chen): pa'i byang chub sems dpa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dang po bskyed pa de ni sangs rgyas mchod pa la sbyar bar bya ste/byang chub sems dpa' bsten par byed pa yang de la brjod par bya'o//
3b4(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi nang na gtso bor gyur pa yang de la brjod par ba'o//phul du phyin pa yang de la brjod par bye'o//zil gyis mi gnon pa'i gnas kyang de la brjod par bya'o//sangs rgyas kyi bdag nyid che ba de la bstan par 
3b5(phal chen): bya'o//sangs rgyas tshad med pa dang 'phrad par 'gyur ba yang de la bstan par bya'o//rab tu zhi ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyi sbyor ba yang de la brjod par bya 'o//'khor ba'i 'khor lo las ldog par 'gyur ba yang de la 
3b6(phal chen): brjod par bya'o//sems can sdug bsngal ba yongs su bskyab pa yang de la brjod par yin pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la de bas kyang shas cher sems bskye bar bya ba dang /gnas gang thos kyang 
3b7(phal chen): de la bdag nyid sbyor bar bya ba dang /bdag nyid gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog par mi 'gyur ba'i bya ba'i phyir te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni sems dang po bskyed pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dgod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i 
3b8(phal chen): sras dag de la las dang po pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dgod pa gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dga' 'di la byang chub sems dpa' thog ma nyid du sems can thams cad la rnam pa bcu sems nye bar gzhog ste/
4a1(phal chen): @#//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste phan pa'i sems nye bar gzhog go/snying rtse ba'i sems dang /bde ba'i sems dang /gnas pa'i sems dang /byams pa'i sems dang /phan 'dogs pa'i sems dang /yongs

4a3(phal chen): dag 'di ni las dang po pa'i byang chub sems dpa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la byang chub sems dpa' las dang po pa de ni thog ma nyid du kha ton la yongs su skul bar bya'o//mang du thos nas dben par gnas pa la yongs 
4a4(phal chen): su bskul bar bya'o//rab tu dben par gyur nas dge ba'i bshes su 'gyur bar yongs su bskul bar bya'o//dge ba'i bshes su gyur nas da lo bde ba la yongs su bskul bar bya'o////po bde bar gyur nas 'du

4a6(phal chen): yongs su bskul bar bya'o/chos kyi rjes su 'gro bar gyur nas rmongs pa med pa la yongs su skul bar bya 'o//rmongs pa med par gyur nas rnam par dgod pa yongs su bskul bar bya'o //de ci'i phyir zhe na/
4a7(phal chen): sems can thams cad la de bas kyang shas cher snying rje chen po mngon du gzhog par bya ba dang //gnas gang thos kyang de la bdag nyid sbyor bar bya ba dang /bdag nyid gzhan gyi drid 'jog par mi 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir te/

4b1(phal chen): la byang chub sems dpa' dmigs pa'i rnam pa bcus chos thams cad la brtson par byed de//bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar chos thams cad la mi rtag pa dang //chos thams cad la sdug bsngal ba dang /chos thams
4b2(phal chen): cad la stong pa nyid dang /chos thams cad bdag med pa dang /chos thams cad byed pa med pa dang /chos thamasacad mod pa med pa dang /chos la thams cad brda' med pa dang /ches thams cad gnas med pa dang /

4b5(phal chen): chos kyi khams brtag pa la yongs 'tshal bar bya'o//'jig rten gyi khams brtag pa la yings su bskul bar bya'o //sa'i khams brtag pa la yongs du bskul bar bya'o//chu'i khams rtag pa la

5a1(phal chen): @#//rnam par dgod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la cher skyes pa'i byang chub sems dpa' gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di la byang chub sems dpa' 'phags pa'i gsung rab la yongs su smin

7b2(phal chen): ba'i sras dag 'di ni phyir mi ldog pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dgod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la gzhon nur gyur pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i gad zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' 
7b3(phal chen): las bcu la gnas pa yin te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar lus kyi spyod pa kha na ma tho ba med pa yin/dag gi spyod pa kha na ma tho ba me pa yin/yid kyi spyod pa kha na ma tho bas med pa yin/sems bskyed 
7b4(phal chen): pa rtsam gyis skye ba bsgrub pa yin/sems kyi bsam pa shes pa yin/gang zag gzhan gyi mos pa rtogs pa yin/sems can gyi khams tha dad pa rab tu shes pa yin/'jig rten gyi khams 'grub 
7b5(phal chen): pa dang 'jig pa yongs su 'jal ba yin/gang dga' bar 'gro ba la myur ba yin/kye rgyal ba'i sras dang /'di ni gzhon nur gyur pa'i byangs chub sems dpa' ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de'i ltar gzhon nur gyur 
7b6(phal chen): pa'i byang chub sems dpa' de la ni chos mngon par bsgrub pa bcu brjod par bya'o//bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su shes pa brjod par bya'o//sangs rgyas kyi zhing rab tu bskyed pa brjod 
7b7(phal chen): par bya'o//sangs rgyas kyi shing gi byin gyi rlabs brjod par bya'o//sangs kyi zhing du rnam par rgyu ba brjod par bya'o//sangs pa rgyas kyi zhing yongs su gnas pa brjod par bya'o/'jig rten gyi khams grangs med 
7b8(phal chen): par 'gro ba brjod par bya'o//chos grangs med pa yongs su dri ba brjod par bya'o/yid kyi rang bzhin gyi lus mngon par bsgrub pa brjod par bya'o/dbyangs kyi sbyor ba rab tu bgyi ba brjod par bya'o/sems bskyed 
8a1(phal chen): @#//pa gcig la sangs rgyas grangs med pa bsnyen bkur bar bya ba brjod par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de bas kyang shas cher chos thams cad la mngon par bsgrub pa'i mkhas pa 'byung bar bya ba dang /
8a2(phal chen): gnas gang thos kyang de la bdag nyid sbyor bar bya ba dang /bdag nyid gzhan gyi dring 'jog par mi 'gyur bar bya ba'i phyir te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni gzhon nur gyur pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dgod pa'o//
8a3(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag rgyal tshab kyi byang chub sems dpa' gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dga' di la byang chub sems dpa'i chos bcu rtogs par bya ba la mkhas pa yin te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar sems can gyi skye 
8a4(phal chen): ba rtogs par bya ba la mkhas/pa yin/nyon mongs pa'i rgyud rtogs par bya ba la mkhasapa yin/bag chags kyi 'chams 'brel ba'i rtogs par bya ba la mkhasab yin/spyod yul rtogs par bya ba la mkhas
8a5(phal chen): chos sna tshogs rtogs par bya ba la mkhas:pa yin/spyad pa rtogs par bya ba la mkhaspa yin/'jig rten gyi khams rnam par phye ba rtogs par bya ba la mkhan pa yin/sngon gyi mtha' dang phyi ma'i mtha' rtogs par bya 
8a6(phal chen): ba la mkhas pa yin/'jig rten gyi kun rdzob kyi bden pa brtag pa rtogs par bya la mkhas pa yin/don dam pa'i bden pa brtag pa rtogasapar bya ba la mkhas pa yin/kye rgyal ba'i sras dang 'di ni rgyal
8a7(phal chen): 'tshab kyi byang chub sems dpa' ste/byang chub sems dpa' rgyal tshab de la ni gnas bcu brjod par bya'o//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/rgyal po'i gnas la mkhas pa'i brjod par bya'o//rgyal po'i gnas
8a8(phal chen): kyi tshul brjod par bya'o//rgyal bo'i gnas su gnas pa brjod par bya'o//rgyal pa'i gnas su 'gro ba brjod par bya'o//rgyal ba'i gnas su spyod pa brjod par bya'o//chos kyi rgyal por dbang skur ba brjod
8b1(phal chen): bar bya'o//chos kyi rgyal po'i byin brjod par bya'o//chos kyi rgyal po mthu brjod par bya'o//chos kyis rgyal srid la gnas pa brjod par bya'o//chos kyi/rgyal po'i sngags pa brjod par bya'o//de ci'i phyir 
8b2(phal chen): zhe na/ci nas kyang de'i sems bsgribs pa med pa mngon par bsgrub pa la de bas kyang shas cher gnas par bya ba dang /gnas gang thos kyang bdag nyid de la sbyor bar bya ba dang /bdag nyid gzhan gyi dring 'jog par mi 
8b3(phal chen): bya ba'i phyir ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni rgyal 'tshab kyi byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dgod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la dbang bskur ba'i byang chub sems dpa' gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag
8b4(phal chen): 'di la byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes la rnam par spyod pa bcu dang ldan pa yin te/bcu gang zhe na 'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams grangs med pa yongs su bskyed pa'i sems mang ba yin/'jig rten gyi khams 
8b5(phal chen): grangs med pa snang bar bya ba'i sems mang ba yin/'jid rten gyi khams grangs med ba'i byin gyis rlab pa'i sems mang ba yin te/'jig rten gyi khams grangs mang ba yin/'jig 
8b6(phal chen): rten gyi khams grangs med na yongs su sbyang ba ji sems mang ba yin/sems can grangs med pa rnam par dmigs par bya ba'i sems mang ba ni/sems can grangs med ba rnam par brtag pa'i sems mang ba yin/
8b7(phal chen): sems can grangs med pa'i dbang po rtogs par bya ba'i sems mad ba yin/sems can grang med pa'i yongs su gzung ba'i sems mad ba yin te/sems can grangs med la gdul ba'i sems mang ba yin te de'i lus ni byang chub 
8b8(phal chen): sems dpa' rgyal tshab la gnas pa'i bar gyis kyang shes par mi nus te lus kyi las shes par mi nus so//rdzu 'phrul gyis shes par mi nus so/rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul pa shes par mi nus so/'das 
9a1(phal chen): @#//pa lta ba 'am shes par mi nus so//ma 'ongs pa lta ba 'am shes par mi nas so//da ltar byung ba lta ba 'am shes par mi nu so//zhing mngon par 'du byed pa shes par mi nus so/sems kyi yul 
9a2(phal chen): shes par mi nus so//ye shes kyi spyod yul yang byang chub sems dpa' rgyal 'tshab la gnas pa'i bar gyis shes par mi nus ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni dbang bskur ba'i byang chub sems dpa'o//
9a3(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' dbang bskur ba de la ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi ye shes la 'jug pa bcu bka' stsal te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar dus gsum gyi ye shes brjod 
9a4(phal chen): par mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi ye shes brjod par mdzad do//chos kyi dbyings chags pa med pa brjod par mdzad do//chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa brjod pa mdzad do//'jig rten gyi khams 
9a5(phal chen): thams cad rgyas par dgang ba brjod pa mdzad do//'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang bar bya ba brjod par mdzad do//'jig rten kyi khams thams cad byin gyis brlab pa brjod pa mdzad do//sems can thams 
9a6(phal chen): cad yongs su shes pa'i ye shes brjod pa mdzad do//chos thams cad yongs su shes pa'i ye shas brjod pa mdzad do//sangs rgyas mtha' yas pa brjod pa mdzad do/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ci nas kyang de'i sems 
9a7(phal chen): rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du de bas kyang shas cher gnas par bya ba dang /gnas gang thos kyang bdag nyid de la sbyor bar bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog par bya ba'i phyir 
9a8(phal chen): te|kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni rgyal 'tshab kyi byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dgod pa'o//de nas de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i brdul shin 
9b1(phal chen): tu phra ba snyed rnam pa drug du g.yos te/g.yos so//rab tu g.yos so//kun tu g.yos so//'gul te rab tu 'gul te/kun tu 'gul to//ldeg go/rab tu ldeg go/kun tu ldeg go/chem chem rab
9b2(phal chen): tu chem chem/kun tu chem chem mo//'ur 'ur rab tu 'ur 'ur kun tu 'ur 'ur ro//shig shig rab tu shig shig kun tu shig shig go/sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang chos nyid thob pas/lha rdzas kyi me tog
9b3(phal chen): gi sprin gyi char mngon par bab po//lha rdzas kyi phye ma'i sprin gyi char mngon par pab po//lha rdzas kyi 'phrad ba'i sprin gyi char mngon parab po//lha rdzas kyi spos kyi sprin gyi char mngon par 'bab po//lha
9b4(phal chen): rdzas kyi gos kyi sprin gyi char mngonapar bab po//lha rdzas kyi rin po che'i sprin gyi char mngon pa rab bo//lha rdzas kyi lhab lhub kyi sprin gyi char mngon bar bab po//lha rdzas kyirol mo'i sgra mngon par byung bar
9b5(phal chen): gyur to ༐ ༐lha rdzas kyi snang ba rab tu byung bar gyur to//lha rdzas kyi nga ro shin tu drag par gyur to//gling bzhi pa 'di'i ri rab kyi rtse mor lha'i dbang po'i khang pa ji lta ba de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 
9b6(phal chen): du yang phyogs bcu rgyas par byung te/chos bstan pa 'di yang thams cad du 'byung ngo//sangs rgyas kyis byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra
9b7(phal chen): ba snyed kyi byang chub sems dpa' mang po lhags ste/phyogs bcu rgyas par bkang nas 'di skad ces 'dzer to//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de ltar khyod byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos nyid de lta bu ston 
9b8(phal chen): pa legs so//legs so//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag bdag cag kun kyang ming chos kyi blo gros shes bya ba sha stag ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan chos kyi blo gros shes bya pa sha stag gi zham ring 'jig
10a1(phal chen): @#//rten gyi khams chos kyi sprin ces bya ba sha stag nas 'dir phyin te//thams cad na yang sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis chos bstan pa 'di nyid 'khor gyi rnam pa 'di lta bu dag tu tshig 'di 
10a2(phal chen): dag dang /yi ge 'di dang /khong du chud par bya ba 'di dag gis don 'di nyid mngon par smos shing lhag chad med par ston te/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de ltar bdag cag dbang du gyur pa sha stag sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis
10a3(phal chen): rlabs kyis 'dir phyin to//ji ltar bdag gcag 'jig rten gyi khams 'dir phyin pa de bzhin du phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi 'jig rten gyi khams re re'i gling bzhi pa dag gi ri rab kyi rtse mo
10a4(phal chen): lha'i dbang po'i khang pa dag tu yang byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed mang po phyin par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros kyis phyogs bcur rnam par 
10a5(phal chen): bltas te chos kyi dbyings la rnam par bltas nas sangs rgyas kyis byin gyis rlabs kyis/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rang 'byung sangs rgyas thugs mchog rab mnga' ba//
10a6(phal chen): sku gzugs sku mdog shin tu mdzes ldan zhing //mtshan bzang thub pa med pa mthong gyur nas//byang chub don du bstan pa sems sgyur ro//dpa' zla med pa'i rdzu 'phrul stobs chen dang //yongs su 
10a7(phal chen): bstan dang rjes su bstan mthong ste//srid lam kun tu sdug bsngal gyur mthong zhing //byang chub don du bstanapa sems sgyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa yon tan kun mnga' ba//kun tu bzang 
10a8(phal chen): po'i yon tan thos gyur cing //nam mkha' dbyings snyam rtog pa med pa yi//brtan pa byang chub don du sems bskyed do//khams gsum kun na gnas ni 'di dag yin//gnas min rang bzhin
10b1(phal chen): dag kyang 'di yin zhes//ma nor dngos po khong du chud bya'i phyir//brtan pa byang chub don du sems bskyed do//'das dang ma 'ongs da ltar byung ba dang //dkar nag las rnams ji snyed yod de dag/
10b2(phal chen): de kun mnyam nyid rjes su rtogs bya'i phyir//brtan pa byangs chub don du sems bskyed de//bsam tan ting 'dzin rnam par thar pa dang //mnyam bzhag de bzhin kun nas nyon mongs dang //rnam byang 
10b3(phal chen): rnam par khong du chud bya'i phyir/brtan pa byang chub don du de sems bskyed do//sems can kun gyi dbang po ci 'dra dang //brtson 'grus stobs dang de bzhin ci 'dra ba//rnam pa sna tshogs 
10b4(phal chen): de dag rtogs bya'i phyir//byang chub don du brtan pa sems sgyur ro//sems can kun gyi mos pa ci 'dra dang //'jig rten kun gyi bsam pa sna tshogs rnams//rnam pa kun tu thams cad rtogs 
10b5(phal chen): bya'i phyir//byang chub don tu brtan pa sems sgyur ro//'gro ba rnams kyi sna tshogs khams rnams dang //'jig rten kun kyi khams mang du ma dang //khams kyi rang bzhin rtogs par bya ba'i phyir//brtan 
10b6(phal chen): pa byang chub don du sems sgyur ro//kun tu 'gro ba'i chos rnams ci 'dra dang //lam rnams ci 'dra 'dus byas 'di dag gis//ma nor rang bzhin rtogs par bya pa'i phyir//byang chub don brtan pa 
10b7(phal chen): sems sgyur ro//zhing rnams thams cad mthong bar bya ba dang //sems can 'di dag ji ltar 'khor gyur ces//chags med mig ni rnam par dag bya'i phyir//brtan pa byang chub don du sems rgyur ro//
10b8(phal chen): 'das pa'i dus su gang rnams ci byung yang //mtshan nyid gang yin ci 'dra ci dngos pa//sngon gyi gnas rnams rjes su rtogs bya'i phyir//brtan pa byangs chub don du sems sgyur ro //'jig rten kun na kun 
11a1(phal chen): @#//sbyor ji snyed dang //don gyi mtshams 'brel bag chags de bzhin te//nyon mongs zad bya mthar thug shes bya'i phyir//byang chub don du brtan pa sems sgyur ro//'gro ba rnams kyi tha snyad gdags pa 
11a2(phal chen): dang //tshig lam skad du brjod pa ji snyed pa//brda'i bden pa rjes su rtogs bya'i phyir//bstan pa byang chub don du sems bsgyur ro//chos rnams ji ltar brjod du med pa dang //stong pa byed med rang bzhin dben 
11a3(phal chen): pa'i rnams//de dag dngos nyid khong du chung bya'i phyir//brtan pa byang chub don du sem bsgyur ro//zhing rnams thams cad rab tu bskyod pa dang //rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu bskam bya bar//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
11a4(phal chen): rdzu 'phrul rtogs ma bya'i phyir//bstan pa byangs chub don du sems sgyur ro//spu rtse re re'i sgo nas 'od zer rnams//phyogs bcu kun tu ji ltar dgye bya ba//de 'dra'i rang bzhin khong du chud bya'i phyir//
11a5(phal chen): bstan pa byang chub don du de sems bsgyur ro//zhing rnams brjod med bsam gyis mi khyab pa//bdag gi lag pa'i thil du gzhag bya bar//sgyu ma'i rnam pa thams cad rtogs bya'i phyir//brtan pa byangs chub 
11a6(phal chen): don du sems bsgyur ro/ji ltar zhing gi sems can yang bkun//gnod pa med par skra rtser gzhog byed par//srog med chos rnams khong du chud bya'i phyir/brtan pa byang chub don du chen gang rgyas pa//skra rtses blangs shing kun tu dor bya bar//mthar thug ma lus khong du chud bya'i phyir//brtan pa byang chub don du sems bsgyur ro//ji ltar bsam gyis mi khyab zhing 
11a7(phal chen): gi rnams//brdul blags ma lus bgrang bya bar//rdul phran grangs rnams khong du chud bya'i yir//bstan pa byang chub don du sem bsgyur ro//'das pa'i bskal pa de bzhin ma 'ongs dang //'jig rten 
11b1(phal chen): che gcig 'grub pa de bzhin te//bskal pa zad cing gnas pa shes bya'i phyir//brtan pa byang chub don du sems bsgyur ro//dus gsum 'byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa dang //rang rgyal nyan thos 
11b2(phal chen): thams cad kyi//chos kyi rang bzhin khong du chud bya'i phyir//brtan pa byangs chub don du sems bsgyur ba//brjod du med pa'i 'jig rten khams kyi rnams//skra'i nyag mas bcings shing chad gdab par//
11b3(phal chen): dngos po'i rang bzhin khong du chud bya phyir//bstan pa byang chub don du sem bsgyur ro//brjod med 'khor yug shin tu yangs pa dag/ba spu'i khung bu ji ltar gzung bya bar//phra dang yangs pa khong du chud bya'i 
11b4(phal chen): phyir//brtan pa byang chub mngon du sems bsgyur ro//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad du//ji ltar dbyangs cig kun tu grags 'gyur bar//dbyangs kyi yan lag shes bya'i phyir//brtan par byang chub don du 
11b5(phal chen): sems bsgyur ro//sems can kun gyi sgra skad ji snyed pa//dbyangs cig brjod pas ji ltar brjod bya bar//sgra'i rang bzhin khong du chud pa'i phyir//brtan pa byang chub don du sems bsgyur//'jig rten thams 
11b6(phal chen): cad ma lus shes byed cing //ji ltar 'gro kun zhi zhes stong 'gyur bar//lce yangs khong du chud bya'i phyir//brtan pa byang chung don du sems sgyur ro//zhing rnams thams cad 'tshig pa dang //kun nas gnas pa ma lus 
11b7(phal chen): mthong 'gyur bar//rnam rtog chos ni khong du chud bya'i phyir/brtan pa byang chub don du de sems sgyur ro//zhing rnam thams cad sangs rgyas gcig bzhin du//de bzhin gshegs pas ji ltar rgyas bya bar//rang byung chos 
11b8(phal chen): ni ji ltar rtogs bya'i phyir//brtan pa byangs chub don du sems sgyur ro/zhing rnams thams cad rdul mchog phra blags pa//de snyed lus su ji ltar sbrul bya bar//thams cad yid kyi dngos su shes bya'i phyir//
12a1(phal chen): brtan pa byang chub don du sems sgyur ro//kun gzigs 'das pa ji snyed byung ba dang //ma byon sangs rgyas de bzhin bzhugs pa rnams//skad cig sems kyis thams cad rtags bya'i phyir//
12a2(phal chen): brtan pa byang chub don du bsems sgyur ro//tshig gcig brtsams cing brjod cing bshad byas kyang //bskal pa brjod med thams cad zad 'gyur bar//chos kyi rnam grangs khong du chud bya'i phyir//brtan pa byang chub 
12a3(phal chen): don du sems sgyur ro//phyogs bcu kun gyi 'jig rten ma lus rnams//'byung zhing ji ltar rjes su 'brang ba dag/sems kyi rang bzhin khong du chud bya'i phyir/brtan pa byang chub don du de bzhin yid kyis kyang //phyogs kun spyod tshe chags par mi 'gyur bar//dus gsum stong pa khong du chud bya'i phyir//rten pa byang chub don du sem sgyur ro//de ltar byang chub don du bskyed 
12a4(phal chen): byed pa//phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas brjod du med pa kun//mchod par bya la yongs su bskul bar bya//'di ni phyir mi ldog gi gdam ngag go/'khor ba dag na byang chub don spyod tshe//byang chub sems dpa'i bde 
12a5(phal chen): ba ji snyed pa//de dag de la yongs su brjod par bya//'di ni phyir mi ldig gi gdam ngag go/'jig rten gtso bo 'jig rten ji snyed pa//phyogs bcu'i tha dad tshad med yod pa rnams//de dag byang chub sems 
12a6(phal chen): dpa' de la brjod//'di ni phyir mi ldog gdam ngag go/mchog gi chos dang ji snyed dam pa'i chos//'phrul gyi chos dang bla na med pa'i chos//de dag byang chub sem dpa' de la bstan//'di ni skyon med 
12a7(phal chen): rnams kyi gdam ngag go/gzhan gyis mi thub ji snyed gnas yod pa//'jig rten kun gyis zil gyis mi non rnams//de la gnas par rgyal sras de brjod de//'di ni phyir mi ldog gyi gdams ngag go/
12b1(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa yon tan kun ldan pa//'jig rten mgon po bdag nyid che ba kun//de la byang chub sems dpa' de bzhag te//'di ni skyes bu khyu mchog gdam ngag go/sangs rgyas grangs med 
12b2(phal chen): brjod du med pa dang //ji ltar 'grogs par 'gyur ba thams cad kyang //byang chub sems dpa' de la brjod par bya//'di ni phyir mi ldog gi gdam ngag go/ting 'dzin zhi ba ji snyed gang yod pa//de dag gdams 
12b3(phal chen): ngag ji snyed ma lus par//byang chub sems dpa' da la bstan par bya//'di ni phyir ma ldog gi gdam ngag go//'khor ba'i 'khor lo ldog par 'gyur ba dang //chos kyi 'khor lo 'khor bar 'gyur ba dang /
12b4(phal chen): 'jig rten kun gyi chags par mi 'gyur ba//byang chub sems dpa' la de la bstan par bya//sems can sdug bsngal zer ba ji snyed dang //ji snyed ngan song gtses par gyur pa yi//skyabs dang mgon dang 
12b5(phal chen): rten du 'gyur ba dang //byang chub sems dba' de la brjod par bya/rnam pa 'di 'dras rgyal sras dang po de//byang chub don du sems rab skyer 'gyur te//de ltar gdam ngag rjes su bstan par bya//'di ni rgyal 
12b6(phal chen): ba rnams kyi rnam pa bkod pa'o//de nas gnyis pa las kyi dang po ba//thag ma nyid du sems can thams cad la//sems bcu nye bar gzhog cing smon byed pa//'di ni skyon dang bral ba'i gdams ngag go/
12b7(phal chen): phan pa'i sems dang de bzhin nges pa'i sems//snying rje'i sems dang de bzhin byams pa'i sems//bsrung ba'i sems dang de bzhin mnyam pa'i sems//slob dpon sems dang de bzhin ston pa'i sems//de 'dra'i 
12b8(phal chen): sems rnams nye bar gzhag pa la//kha ton lung la yongs su bskul bar bya//mang du thos pa dgon par gnas par bskul//dben par gyur pas dge bshes bsnyen 'gyur bya//dge bshes gyur nas dad bder 'gyur

13b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas rnams la shin tu mos pa yin//chos rnams de bzhin nges par rtogs pa te//sems can rang bzhin med par rnam par bsgom//chos dang 'jig rten de bzhin zhing rnams dang //rnam smin 'khor 
13b2(phal chen): ba de bzhin mya ngan 'das//rnam bsgom rgyal sras de ltar tsher skyes pa//thugs las byung ba'i sangs rgyas sras shes bya//'das dang ma byon da ltar byung ba yi//sangs rgyas chos rnams rnam par bsgom bya'i phyir//
13b3(phal chen): yongs su sdu ba ci 'dra mthar thug pa//da ltar de dag 'grub par 'gyur ba dang //sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam pa nyid rtogs shing //dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi rjes song bas//mnyam pa nyid la sna tshogs 
13b4(phal chen): mi dmigs shing //dus gsum kun la tha dad med par 'jug/de tsam gyis na rgyal sras bzhi pa ste//de'i sngags pa rnam pa de 'dra'o//de ltar 'di la nan tan bsgrub byas shing //byang chub sems 
13b5(phal chen): dpa' byang chub mchog du 'byung //de las gzhan yang rgyal sras lnga pa ni//sbyor ba phun sum tshogs zhes brjod pa ni//thabs mkhas tshul la rjes su zhugs gyur cing //bsod nams las rnams rtsams pa'i 
13b6(phal chen): mthar phyin byed//'jig rten dag na bsod nams gang byed pa//sems can kun gyi skyabs su 'gyur ba dang //phan tshun de bzhin bde ba bskyed pa dang //snying rjes skye dgu grol bar 'gyur ba dang //'jig rten khams 
13b7(phal chen): kyi gtso bo gsal ba dang //drang zhing de bzhin dang bar bya ba dang //sems can mang po thams cad gdul mos shing //mya ngan 'das pa'i sems kyis mthu yang che//sems can thams cad kun tu mtha' yas dang //

14b1(phal chen): rnam phye phan tshun yongs su sbyar pa rnams//thos nas 'di la mthar thug 'jug par byed//gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' gzhon gyur pa//lus dang ngag dang de bzhin yid kun gyis//kha na ma thong 
14b2(phal chen): 'gyur ba nam yang med//ci 'dod tshul bzhin de ni skye ba byed//gzhan gyi bsam pa ji bzhin rab tu shes//de dag mos pa yongs su rtogs par byed//phyogs rnams gang du yid kyis smon byed pa//kun tu 
14b3(phal chen): mngon shes myur ba yid bzhin 'thob//bde bar gshegs pa'i sngags pa'i chos thos shing //de la zhum pa bskyed pa gang yang med//zhing rnams yongs su skyod pa shes pa dang //de bzhin byin rlabs rnam 
14b4(phal chen): par rgyu ba dang //sangs rgyas zhing rnams yongs su gnan pa dang //grangs med kun tu 'gro ba brjod par bya//grangs med chos rnams yongs su 'gri pa dang //ci nas ci'i rang bzhin lus 'gyur zhing //

14b8(phal chen): spyad brjod//de la chos kyi rgyal po'i dbang skur dang //byin gyis rlabs dang de bzhin mthu che dang //chos su gnas dang 'gro bar bya ba dag/brjod par bya ste rgyal 'tshab de 'dra'i yin//de tsam gyis 
15a1(phal chen): @#//na de'i mthar thug ste//gdam ngag des kyang rlom sems byed pa med//khong du chud pa byed ldan rgyal 'tshab de//rgyal ba kun gyi drung na slob pa byed//bde bar gshegs pa'i sras po dbang bskur ba//
15a2(phal chen): thu bo mchog dang dam par gyur pa ni//'jig rten khams mang grangs med skyob pa dang //yongs su snang bar bya ba'i sems kyang med//byin gyis rlab dang yongs su gda' bya dang //yongs su sbyang ba'i sems 
15a3(phal chen): kyang rab tu med//grangs med 'jig rten shes par bya ba dang //dbang po phun ldan rnam par spyad la mkhas//de dag gzung cing rnam par 'dul ba dang //thams cad mkhyen pa'i sems kyang shin tu mang //
15a4(phal chen): zhing mang brjod du med par 'gro byas shing //dam chos khams rnams ma lus rnam par spyod//de'i lus dang lus kyi las rnams dang //rdzu 'phrul bstan pa'ang shes par mi nus ste//dus gsum 
15a5(phal chen): zhing du rgyu ba'i sems dag kyang //rgyal 'tshab 'gyur ba'i bar gyis shes mi nus//de la thams cad gzigs pas dus gsum dang //yongs su snang byed byin gyis rlob pa dang //sems can chos rnams 
15a6(phal chen): de bzhin ma lus dang //sangs rgyas mtha' yas de bzhin brjod pa mdzad//rgyal ba sras pa stan pa rnam pa bcur grangs pa//rnam par dgod la gnas pa de dag kun//rang rang spyod yul rab tu brtson byed pa//
15a7(phal chen): 'jig rten lhar bcas kun gyis shes par bka'//'on kyang sems pa skyed dang por mtha' yas pas//phyogs bcu ma lus thams cad rgyas par bya//dus gsum kun tu gang tshe sems gtong ba//

15b1(phal chen): sems rab de la gnas pa yin//skad gcig sems la byang chub gzhol pa'i sems//gang skyes 'jig rten kun gyis shes mi nus//rang rgyal rnams dang nyan thos kun gyis min//de nas gong du spyod 
15b2(phal chen): pa ji skad brjod//spu cig gang zhig phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten khams//spu cig srad la tshad du 'jal byed pa//des ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa shes//'on kyang sangs rgyas ye shes 
15b3(phal chen): yongs su tshol//phyogs bcu'i rgya mtsho ji snyed yod pa kun//spu rtse'i thigs kyis yongs 'jal shes byed pa//de ni rgyal sras spyod pa 'di 'dra dag/sems kyis skad cig gcig la sgrub par byed//

15b5(phal chen): gang rnams phyogs bcur 'byung bar 'gyur ba dang //rang rgyal de bzhin nyan thos rab mang ba//rnam par dmigs shing byang chub sems skye'o//sems can khams rnams ma lus rgyas gang zhing //byang chub don du thog mar sems 
15b6(phal chen): bskyed pa//de yang bgrang bar nus pa ma yin na//de las gong du ci zhig smos kyang dgos//byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dgod pa bcu bstan pa'i le'u ste/nyi shu pa'o////

30a2(phal chen): dang /glu dang rin po che'i dra ba dang /gser gyi dra ba dang /nor bu'i dra ba dang /dril bu'i dra ba dang /seng ge'i khri dang /de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi mchod pa brtsams pa rgya cher ste/de bzhin gshegs pa de dag bzhugs

30a7(phal chen): zad par 'gyur te/shar phyogs su ji lta ba de bzhin du lho phyogs dang /nub phyogs dang /byang phyogs dang /byang shar gyi phyogs 'tshams dang /shar lho'i phyogs 'tshams dang /lho nub kyi phyogs 'tshams dang /

41b7(phal chen): ting nge 'dzin dang gzungs kyi dam par rab tu brgyan//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam 'phrul rnam pas brgyan//'jig rten kun dang 'gro ba rnams kyi ji tsam tshad//nam mkha'i tshad kyang phyogs

42a1(phal chen): @#//bzhi'i gnas yin so sor shes pa'i rtse//rgyal ba'i chos rnams bco brgyad yang dag 'byin pa ste//skyes bu'i gtso bos mkhyen par bya ba'i chos kun 'byin//gzugs 
42a2(phal chen): kyi sku ni mtshan rab rnams kyis bstsags pa dang //chos sku snyam nyid rtog pa mi mnga' brnyes pa dang //'gro bas mchod pa'i ye shes chags med gang byung ba//kun gyi rtsa bas 'byung gnas byang chub 
42a3(phal chen): sems 'di yin//rang rgyal theg pa 'byung zhing nyan thos theg pa 'ang 'byung //bsam gtan ting 'dzin bde ba gzugs kyi khams rnams dang //gzugs med khams dang ting nge 'dzin gyi bde ba dag/
42a4(phal chen): kun gyi rtsa ba byang chub sems mchog 'di yin no//mi dang de bzhin lha'i dbang po bde ba dang //lam rgyud sna tshogs kun kyang 'di la shin tu sbyangs//brtson 'grus stobs dang dbang po'i bde ba gang yod pa//
42a5(phal chen): kun gyi rtsa ba dang po byang chub sems 'di yin//ci phyir zhe na 'di ltar byang chub sems bskyed cing //pha rol phyin drug brtson par spyad pa rab tu spyod//sems can rnams la'ang spyod pa yang dag rab 
42a6(phal chen): tu ston//des ni khams gsum 'gro ba'i bde ba kun kyang 'byung //sangs rgyas chags med thugs chud don dam mkhyen pa'i rnams//chos kyi dam pa'i mchog rnams yang dag kun ston cing //
42a7(phal chen): rgya chen nyon mongs las las sems can grol mdzad de//mya ngan 'das pa dam pa mchog la dgod mdzad pa//ye shes 'od zer 'od yangs nyi ma lta bu ste//'gro bar spyod pa dkar pos yongs rgyas 
42a8(phal chen): grub bzhin//yon tan shin tu bsags pa tshad med chu lta bu//dri med chags pa mi mnga' tshad med mkha' dbyings 'dra//gang zhig yon tan mtha' yas byang chub sems yangs pa//shin tu che zhib 
42b1(phal chen): bde bskyed tshad med smon byed cing//phyi ma'i mthar thug spyod par smon pas su brtson pa//de dag sems can gdul ba'i don phyir rtag tu brtson//'jig rten 'byam klas thams cad rnam par snang 
42b2(phal chen): ba'i phyir//smon lam bye ba khrag khrig bsam gyis mi khyab dang//stong pa mtshan ma med pa smon med mi gnas pa'i//smon lam bye ba brgyas ni sems kyi bag chags dag par 'gyur//chos kyi rang 
42b3(phal chen): bzhin nam mkha' lta bu sus rtogs shing//zhi dang rab zhi nyer bzhir thams cad mnyam gzhog pa//de dag chos grangs khrag khrig bsam gyis mi khya ba pa'i//grangs su 'gro la ston kyang de ni gnas pa med//
42b4(phal chen): phyogs bcu kun gyi 'jig rten gtso bo ji snyed pa//de dag kun gyi byang chub sems mchog 'di sngags ste//yon tan brgya phrag bsam gyis mi khyab pas kyang brgyan//sems can rtso bzhin yongs dag pha rol 
42b5(phal chen): phyin pa yin//bskal pa sems can snyed du legs brjod brjod nas kyang //byang chub sems rab de la mthar thug gang yang med//rgyal ba 'byung ba'i rin chen der ni gang skyes pa//de'i dpe ni khams gsum 
42b6(phal chen): glongs dang gang yang med//dus gsum rgyal ba kun gyis gang zhig bstsags pa'i//byang chub sems rab del smon lam kun gyi bgyis//byang chub sems rab dam pa de ni bsod nams gter//
42b7(phal chen): des ni rgyal sras chags pa med pa rab tu 'thob//'gro ba'i sems dang spyod pa shes shing mthong rung srid//zhing rnams brdul du blags te bgrang bar nus kyang srid//bar snang skra yis mnyam par gzhal 
42b8(phal chen): bar nus srid kyi//byang chub sems mchog yon tan mthar phyin mi nus so//'di las dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad rab tu byung //'di las 'jig rten 'gro ba kun gyi bde ba 'byung //
43a1(phal chen): 'di las rgyal bas sngags pa'i legs pa thams cad 'byung //'dis ni sems can gtso la the tshom med par 'gyur//'dis ni 'jig rten kun gyi sgribs pa zad par 'gyur//
43a2(phal chen): 'dis ni zhing rnams yongs su dag pa'i mchog tu 'gyur//'di las 'jig rten dam pa shes dang ldan pa 'byung //gang zhig phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba lta bar 'dod pa dang //gang zhig byin dang 
43a3(phal chen): bsod nams mi zad mdzod 'dod dang //gang zhig 'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal zlog 'dod pa//nges ni yon tan mtha' yas byang chub sems bskyed bya////byang chub sems dpa' sems dang po bskyed 
43a4(phal chen): pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po'i dpe' yang dag par bstsags pa/tshigs su gcad pa'i le'u zhes bya ba ste/nyi shu gnyis so////
43a5(phal chen): kham po bzhi pa////de nas byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus blo gros gyis byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa'
43a6(phal chen): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems bskyed pa rab tu thob pa/sems dang po bskyed pa la gnas pa/de ltar tshad med pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs dang ldan pa//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rgyun chen po la 
43a7(phal chen): yang dag par mdzes pa//byang chub sems dpa'i skyon med par shin tu chud pa/'jig rten gyen tu khrid par byed pa/rnam par bzlog pa rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi 'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos nyid kyi rjes su 
43a8(phal chen): song ba//'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa//byang chub sems pa'i rnam par dgod pa la shin tu nges pa//rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu rab tu 
43b1(phal chen): gzhol ba de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i bstan pa ji ltar brtson pa byas pa'i rnam par byas pa'i rnam pa gang gis/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes par 'gyur ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dgod pa 
43b2(phal chen): las 'das par 'gyur ba dang //spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po yongs su dag par 'gyur ba dang /smon lam chen po yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba dang /byad chub sems dpa'i gter chen po rab tu thob par 'gyur 
43b3(phal chen): ba dang /sems can rnams la gnod ji bzhin du sbyin par byed par 'gyur ba dang /pha rol tu phyin pa'i phyogs yongs su mi gtong bar 'gyur ba dang /ji ltar dam pa ces pa'i sems can rnams sgrol bar 'gyur ba dang /
43b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas dang chos dang dge 'dun gdung mi chad par 'gyur ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba dmigs pa thams cad du sbyor ba don med par mi 'gyur ba dang //kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis 
43b5(phal chen): de la brtson par byas pas chos kyi yul 'di dag yongs su rdzogs par 'gyurabar/ji ltar brtsam par bya ba de legs par bshad pa mdzod cig/'khor 'di dag thams cad ni nyan par 'dod do/
43b6(phal chen): sems can chen po de dag brtson pa'i rnam pa ji lta bus ma rig pa'i mun nag thams cad brtsal te/bdud kyis dkrugs pa'i 'khor thams cad las shin tu rgyal bar byas nas phas kyi rgol ba thams cad zil gyis 
43b7(phal chen): mnan te/sems kyi dri ma kun nas nyon mongs pa'i phyogs thams cad kun nas gsal nas//dge ba'i rtsa ba phun sum tshogs pa thams cad shin tu bsgrubs te/mi khom pa dang ngan song dang /ngan 'gro'i lam 
43b8(phal chen): thams cad rnam par bzlog nas/ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor chen po rnam par byang bar byas te/byang chub sems pa'i sa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa dang /gzugs dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /mngon par shes 
44a1(phal chen): @#//pa dang /rig pa dang /mi 'jigs pa dang yongs su dag pa rnams mngon par bsgrubs nas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad rnam par dag pa dang /mtshan dang dpe' byad bzang po dang /
44a2(phal chen): sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi brgyan mngon par bsgrub yongs su rgyas par byas ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi stobs dang /mi 'jigs pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa mnyam pa nyid kyi 
44a3(phal chen): rjes su song ste/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi yul rtogs par 'gyur ba dang /de ltar mngon par byang chub nas kyang bsam pa'i tshul bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su grub par gyur pa dag tu 
44a4(phal chen): ji ltar yongs su smin par bya ba'i sems can dag la/dus dang dbang ba'i rgyud ji lta ba bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa mtha' yas pa'i byin par 'gyur ba de legs par shog cig/'khor 'di dag/
44a5(phal chen): thams cad ni nyan par 'gyur ro//ji ltar yang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis/byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan 'di dang gzhan dag dang /byang chub sems dpa'i chos dang /
44a6(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /byang chub sems pa'i lam gyi yul rnams yongs su rdzogs par ma byas te/myur du de bzhin gshegs pa'i mnyam pa nyid 'thob pa dang /de bzhin gshegs 
44a7(phal chen): pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis bskal pa rgya stong grangs med par bsgrubs pa'i chos kyi mdzod 'di yang dag par bsrung zhing /byang chub sems dpa'ispyod pa la gnas shing 
44a8(phal chen): ston pa dang 'chad pa'i tshe/bdud dang bdud kyi rigs su rtogs pa dang /phas kyi rgol ba thams cad kyis zil gyis non pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du yang skyob med 
44b1(phal chen): par dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin par byed pa dang /de yang dag par ston pa'i tshe/lha'i dbang po rnams kyis rim gror bya ba dang /gzhung de bzhin du klu'i dbang po dang /gnod 
44b2(phal chen): sbyin gyi dbang po dang /dri za'i dbang po dang /lha ma yin gyi dbang po dang /nam mkha' lding gi dbang po dang /mi'am ci'i dbang po dang /lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po dang /mi'i dbang po dang /
44b3(phal chen): tshangs pa'i dbang pos rim gror byas la rab tu 'thob pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi dbang pos rim gro bya ba la rab tu 'thob pa dang de ltar rim gror byas nas kyang /'jig rten 
44b4(phal chen): 'jig rten thams cad kyis phyag bya ba/'jig rten thams cad kyis mngon par mchod pa/'jig rten thams cad gyis dbang skur ba/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyi dgongs 
44b5(phal chen): pa/byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs kyis shin tu bsrungs ba/rang gi dge ba'i rtsa ba dang /smon lam rab tu thob pa/chos dkar po chen po yang dag par spel ba'i rnams/
44b6(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos pas mdzod rnam par 'byed par 'gyur ba dang /dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pa rgya chen po rab tu 'byed pa dang /
44b7(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i rim gyis yongs su bsgrub pa/shin tu ston par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i gnas dag yang dag par 'gyur ba mdzod cig/de nas byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus blo gros kyis don 'di nyid bstan 
44b8(phal chen): te brjod pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros la tshigs su bcad de smras pa//spyod la 'jug pa mtha' yas yangs pa dag/rang 'byung ye shes bsgrub pa'i dge ba'i rnams//rdzogs pa'i byang 
45a1(phal chen): @#//chu ba sems dpa' yon tan can//grags pa chen po rnams la legs par shod//byang chub sems dpa' gang rnams sems thob cing//bsod nams ldan pa ye shes thob pa'i rnams//
45a2(phal chen): skyon med zhugs pa 'jig rten rnams bzlog cing //yang dag sangs rgyas chos mnyam nyid thob rnams//de dag gis ni bde gshegs bstan pa la//brtson 'grus bstan las ji ltar brtson par bya//gang gis phyogs 
45a3(phal chen): bcu'i rgyal ba mnyes byas shing//yang dag sangs rgyas sa yang gnan bya dang//spyod pa dag dang smon lam yongs dag dang //ye shes gter chen rab tu thob 'gyur zhing //thob nas de dag 'gro kun mi gnas pa//
45a4(phal chen): yongs su blangs pa rtag du gtong byed dang //gang yang pha rol phyin pa mchog gi lam//de dag mi stong rab tu brjod pa dang //sems can ji ltar sgrol byed dam bcas shing //yang dag sangs rgyasbsam pa 
45a5(phal chen): mnyam 'dzin dang//mig gi dmigs pa stan pa thams cad kyi//'byung ba yon tan can la stobs 'gyur zhing //skyes bu khyu mchog de dag brtson gyur pa'i//rnam dag lam de khyu mchog khyod kyis bston//
45a6(phal chen): ji ltar de dag mun pa kun gsal cing//'khrug pa'i 'khor rnams thams cad phan byas nas//dri ma bral ba'i dge ldan mkhas pa rnams//skyes bu khyu mchog ye shes ldan 'gyur dang //mi khom ngan
45a7(phal chen): song lam rnams yongs spangs shing //ye shes chen po'i dkyil 'khor mchog sbyong dang//yon tan bzang po thams cad ldan gyur cing//rgyal ba'i ye shes stobs dang thob 'gyur dang//
45a8(phal chen): skyes bu khyu mchog ye shesazhugs nas kyang //sems can bsam pa ji bzhin ston byed cing //mtha' yang yangs pa'i zhing rnams bsam yas su//ston pa chen po'i las kyang byed pa dang//
45b1(phal chen): ci nas de dag spyad pa mchog thob nas//mi'i khyu mchog mdzod rnams ston byed de//phal gyis mi non gzhan gyis mi 'pham par//rgyal ba'i chos nyid rtag tu 'dzin byed ston//
45b2(phal chen): seng ge bzhin du ji ltar 'jigs pa med//khyu mchog mdzod bzhin dag pa'i mdog du spyod//chu la pad mo bzhin du chags med pa//de dag rgyal ba'i yon tan ji ltar spyod//
45b3(phal chen): de skad ces smras pa dang //byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros kyis//byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus blo gros la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras khyod ni skye bo mang po la 
45b4(phal chen): phan pa dang //skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'jig rten la snying brtse ba dang /lha dang mi rnams kyi don dang /phan pa dang /bde ba'i phyir bzhugs pa ste//kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar 
45b5(phal chen): khyod byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa shin tu rtags shing /rnam par dag pa brtsams te/'dri zhing de ltar rgyal ba'i sras khyod//de bzhin gshegs pa dri ba chos mang po la rab tu gnas pa//
45b6(phal chen): brtson 'grus chen pos shin tu bstsags pa/brtson 'grus ldog pa med pas shin tu 'byung ba 'dri bar byed pa ni//kye rgyal ba'i sras legs so legs so//kye rgyal ba'i sras/de'i phyir 
45b7(phal chen): shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang /sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis phyogs tsam bstan par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di la byang chub sems dpa' 
45b8(phal chen): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du sems bskyed pa thob pa rnams kyis thog mar sems bskyed pa de mi nyams par yongs su bsrung ba dang /rim gror bya ba'i phyir bag yod par bya'o//kye rgyal ba yi sras de la rnam pa bcus na byang chub 
46a1(phal chen): @#//sems dpa' bag yod pa gnas pa shes bya ste//bcu gang zhe na tshul khrims kyi phung po shes pa dang /byang chub kyi sems mnyams shing shin tu sbyangs pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' bsam
46a2(phal chen): pa g.yo dang sgyu med pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba sbyor ba phyir mi ldog pa dang /rang gi sems bskyed pa shin tu rtogs pa dang /phyogs khyim pa dang /so so'i skye bo dang ma 'dres pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa

46b1(phal chen): mthon dman dang bral ba yin//sbyor ba bzang ngan dang bar ma la sems nyams pa med pa yin//sems can kun gyi dang na thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du sems gcig bskyed pa la'ang

46b4(phal chen): brtsams pa/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa dang /mos pa che ba dang /rnal 'byor rgyun ma bcad pa chos thams cad la mi gnas pa/chos thams cad la brtson pa/nyon mongs pa med pa'i tshul la zhugs 
46b5(phal chen): pa/sems yangs pa sangs rgyas kyi chos mtha' dang dbus med pa'i rjes su song ba rnams kyis ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams mnyes par 'gyur

46b7(phal chen): dang /sus dang srog la mi lta ba dang /'tsho ba'i yo byad dang /'tsho ba'i rkyen la don du gnyer ba med pa dang /chos thams cad rnam par bsgom pas nam mkha' dang mnyam pa dang 
46b8(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings la yang dag par gzhol ba'i lhag mthong la mkhas pa dang /mi gnas pa'i chos rnam par bsgom pa'i phyag rgya dang /rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par bsgrubs pa'i smon lam chen po dang /
47a1(phal chen): @#//yongs su sbyangs pa'i bzod pa shes pa'i snang ba dang /rang gi dge ba'i chos bstsags pa dang /ma bstsags pa shin tu brtags pa dang /mngon par 'du bya ba med pa'i sgo shin tu byang ba ste/

47a4(phal chen): gnas pa dang /spyod pa yongs su rdzogs par byas pa la gnas pa dang /smon lam mngon par bsgrub pa gnas pa dang /thabs mkhas pa la gnas pa dang /brtan pa'i stobs kyi shugs la gnas pa dang /chos

47a7(phal chen): pa dang /pha rol tu phyin rnam par phye ba tshad med pa'i las gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog pa'i ye shes snang ba dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang 'grogs pa rgyun mi chad pa dang /brtson 'grus zhig pa med pa dang /de bzhin

47b2(phal chen): dag gis byang chub sems dpa' rnams/sa nas sar rnam par dgod pa las 'da' ba yin no//kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' de dag ni thog ma nas/sa rnam par dgod pa thams cad la mkhas pa rnam

48a1(phal chen): @#//ba'i sras de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis rnam pa bcus spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongs su sbyang bar byas te/bcu gang zhe na yo byad yongs su gzung ba med pa dang /'gro ba thams cad tshim

48a4(phal chen): thams cad dang ma 'dres pa'i mu stegs su gyur pa dang /'gro ba thams chad du sangs rgyas thams cad dang /rgyud kyi tshul gcig tu gyur pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras rnam pa bcu po de dag gis/byang chub sems dpa'

48b1(phal chen): dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras rnam pa bcu po de dag gis/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongs su sbyang bar bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam rnam par dge ba bcu

59a2(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' 'ongs par gyur pa de bzhin du/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa kun tu yang /rab mtshe ma'i gnas kyi khang pa nor bu sna tshogs su yang lhags par gyur te/byang chub 
59a3(phal chen): sems dpa'i ming yang de skad ces bya ba sha stag/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan kyang de skad ces bya ba'i zham ring na/tshangs par spyod pa sha stag go/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis zhabs kyi bol gnyis 
59a4(phal chen): nas 'od zer bye ba brgya stong bkye ste/phyogs bcu thams cad snang bar byas nas/'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad bstan te/byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis/'jig rten gyi khams tha mas cad kyi gling 
59a5(phal chen): bzhi pa kun gyi rab mtshe ma'i gnas thams cad na yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa yod par mthong ngo //de nas byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur 
59a6(phal chen): rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas 'od zer 'od snang bas//phyogs rnams thams cad rnam par snang //des ni rab tu gsal byas pas//
59a7(phal chen): mig la rab rib gang yang med//mtshe ma rab kyi gnas 'di na//gang zag dam pa sangs rgyas bzhugs//de bzhin kun na'ang gsal bar snang //de ni 'jig rten ngo mtshar to//sems 
59a8(phal chen): can gtso bo bcu'i rnams//gshegs par gyur pas mngon par bstod//de dag 'khor kyang ci 'dra ba//de bzhin kun na rab tu snang //phyogs rnams bcu po thams cad na//chos kyi dam pa rab tu ston//
59b1(phal chen): de kun ming yang 'di bzhin te//bdag cag rnams kyi kun rdzob bzhin//'jig rten khams kyi ming dag kyang //de zhes bya ba kun nas 'os//gang zag dam pa de dag ni//tshangs pa'i spyod pa de na spyod//bdag 
59b2(phal chen): cag rnams la gang brtags pa//kun gyi ming yang de dang mthun//sangs rgyas zhing rnams 'phrul ldan pa//de dag sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul 'o//mi'i 'jig rten kun na'ang yang //'di ni rgyal ba bzhugs par 
59b3(phal chen): shes//gzhan gyis de'i rgyal po'i//gnas na de bzhin bzhugs pa mthong //'jig rten khams rnams thams cad na//gang zag dam pa de bzhugs kyang //rgyal ba mtshe ma rab gnas na//bzhugs par bdag cag 
59b4(phal chen): kun kyang sems//'jig rten khams rnams/thams cad na//sangs rgyas gzi brjid bsam mi khyab//de dag smon lam bskul ba'i phyir//'jig rten gyi khams su rab tu bkye//sems can gtso bo'i rnam 
59b5(phal chen): 'phrul pas//'di dag de rnams kun gyis mthong //sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' yas ste//'jig rten zang zing thub pas spangs//phyogs rnams thams cad kun tu rgyu//chags pa med pa nam mkha' bzhin//
59b6(phal chen): de ni gcig las shin tu 'das//mang po dag tu'ang mi dmigs so//mtha' yas legs mnga' sangs rgyas rnams//de dag shes par ji ltar rung //gnas pa med cing len pa med//chos dbyings 
59b7(phal chen): rtogs par thugs su chud//de nas byang chub sems dpa' blo gros dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag 
59b8(phal chen): smras so//'jig rten mgon po 'phrul chen rnams//rdul med gang zag dam pa dag/'dus shing tshogs pa bsam yas pa'i//bskal pa dag tu shin tu dkon//de dag thams cad kun 
60a1(phal chen): @#//gzigs pa//sems can gtso bo 'od mdzad de//srog chags kun la phan pa'i phyir//sems can rnams la chos ston to//'jig rten mun nag 'di dag tu//'jig rten 
60a2(phal chen): mgon po byung gyur pa//'jig rten sgron ma de dag ni//blta bar shin tu dka' ba yin//sbyin pa tshul khrims bzod pa'i rab//brtson 'grus bsam gtan bsgom pa dang //shes rab 
60a3(phal chen): sngon chad spyad pas na//'gro ba thams cad snang ba mdzad//sangs rgyas 'dra ba gang yang med//de phyir de dang mnyam pa med//chos rnams yod pa ma yin pas//skyes bu dam pa mthong ba med//
60a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas shes rab chen po rnams//mtshan gyis tshogs ni bsam mi khyab//sku'i tshogs kyis shin tu 'phags//chos rnams kun gyis yang dag byung //'jig rten grags chen dag pa rnams//dpa' bo chen 
60a5(phal chen): po de dag snang //de dag mtshan tsam thos pas kyang //ngan song thams cad spangs par 'gyur//bskal pa rnams ni brjod med par//byang chub don du 'khor byas kyang //'di'i tshul ni ma shes na//byang 
60a6(phal chen): chub don du zhugs pa min//sangs rgyas bsam gyis mi khyab pa//bskal pa bye bar bkur ba dang //gang gis tshul 'di bsams pa gnyis//'di ni de las khyad du 'phags//gang gis 
60a7(phal chen): brjod byas zhing gi rnams//rin chen gang ste thub gsol kyang //'di'i tshul ni mar shes na//sangs rgyas byang chub de la ring //de nas byang chub sems dpa' dpal dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi byin 
60a8(phal chen): gyi rlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//nam mkha'i dri ma med pa la//nyi ma'i 'od zer mtha' yas pa//dpyid kyi zla ba tha ma la//phyogs bcu dag tu rab 
60b1(phal chen): tu snang //de tshe smig rgyu ci 'dra ba//de dag bgrang bar mi nus te//mig can rnams kyang de la rmongs//long ba rnams kyi spyod yul min//de bzhin sems can gtso bo yi//yon tan mtha' dang dbung med pa//
60b2(phal chen): bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par//sus kyang shes par mi nus so//de dag gang nas blangs pa med//de ni ci las skyes pa med//rnam par bsgoms na yod pa min//'ongs pa med pa'i chos rnams 
60b3(phal chen): la//'gro ba de bzhin mi dmigs ste//de phyir de dag rgyu yang med//de bzhin 'jig pa'ang yod pa min//'jig pa med pa'i chos rnams kyi//rang bzhin gang gis shes pas ni//gang la skye ba 
60b4(phal chen): med pa yi//yang dag sangs rgyas mthong ba yin//skyes pa med pa'i chos rnams kyis//rang bzhin dngos po mi dmigs te//de 'dra'i don tu shes pas ni//rang bzhin dmigs pa med pa yin//'di la 
60b5(phal chen): shes byed gang yang med//chos rnams thams cad mtshan nyid med//chos nyid 'di ni shes gyur la//yongs su shes pa gang yang med//skye ba dag ni brjod pa ste//de phyir zhing rnams rab 
60b6(phal chen): du brjod//zhing gi rang bzhin shes gyur pa//'khrug pa med pa rab tu shes//gang tshe 'jig rten zhing yin par//tshul bzhin du ni rtogs byed na//de tshe thams cad don bstan pa//
60b7(phal chen): byang chub sems dpas rab tu shes//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dga' ba dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras 
60b8(phal chen): so//gnas 'di shin tu rgya che ste//chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa yin//rtag tu kun tu song ba ste//yid du 'ong zhing gnas kyang med//de la yang dag brtag byas shing //
61a1(phal chen): @#//rim gro yod par dang byed na//sdug bsngal nam yang mi mthong ste//ngan song rnams kyang mi mthong ngo//tshad med de bzhin grangs med pa'i//'jig rten khams su su 'gro bas//gang 
61a2(phal chen): rnams 'di na snang ba yi//sangs rgyas byin rlabs thos par 'gyur//drang srong chen po'i byin gyi rlabs//gang gis thos par gyur nas kyang//'di 'dra sangs rgyas chos so zhes//rtog med bla med 
61a3(phal chen): byang chub po//gang rnams 'das pa'i sangs rgyas kyi//chos nyid la ni yid ches pas//de kun 'jig rten sgron ma ste//rkang gnyis ldan pa'i dam par gyur//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul ba 'di//la la 
61a4(phal chen): zhig gis thos gyur cing//thos nas dad par byed gyur na//de dag mi'i dam par 'gyur//brtan pa gang rnams da ltar yang//sangs rgyas chos la dang byed pa//de dag don gzigs mi 'jigs pa'i//de bzhin gshegs 
61a5(phal chen): par 'gyur pa yin//bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par//'di 'dra thos pa shin du dkon//smon lam dbang ni ma gtogs par//blo gros 'di 'dra 'gyur ba min//sangs rgyas dam chos 'di las kyang//legs 
61a6(phal chen): par bshad pa gzung byas shing/gzhan la ston pa su byed pa//de dag bsam gyis mi khyab 'gyur//de la brtson 'grus bskyed byas shing//yid la go cha bgo byas te//gang las sangs rgyas brjod pa yi//
61a7(phal chen): shes pa 'di 'dra mnyan par bya//de nas byang chub sems dpa' khrel dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smras pa//gang zhig thos 
61a8(phal chen): nas 'di 'dra ba'i//dga' ba yangs pa skye ba dang //the tsom rnam par gcad pa ni//'jig rten na ni ngo mtshar che//'di ltar thams cad gzigs pa'i rnams//nyid kyis 'gro la bka' stsal na//sangs rgyas 
61b1(phal chen): kyi mkhyen gang yang med//de ltar sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//mi shes pa'i rgyu las ni//ye shes 'jug par mi 'gyur te//mun pa'i mthu dang snang ba gnyis//lhan cig 'byung bar mi 'gyur 
61b2(phal chen): bstan//ji ltar gzugs dang gzugs med pa//bskal pa mnyam pa med par bshad//de ltar shes dang mi shes pa//'grogs par shin tu mi 'gyur ro//'khor dang mya ngan 'das pa dag/ji ltar med 
61b3(phal chen): las brtags pa ltar//de bzhin shes dang mi shes pa//mtshan nyid bzhin du dmigs pa med//ji ltar 'jig rten 'jig pa dang //'du bar 'gyur ba ring ba ste//phan tshun 'grogs par mi 'gyur ba//shes 
61b4(phal chen): dang mi shes de bzhin no//ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' yi//thog ma'i sems dang tha ma'i sems//lhan cig mi gnas 'byung ba ltar//shes dang mi shes de bzhin no/ji ltar lus dang rnam 
61b5(phal chen): shes pa//gzhan dang gzhan las 'byung 'gyur ba//de bzhin shes dang mi shis gnyis//'grogs par 'gyur ba nam yang med//ji ltar dug la phan pa'i rtsi//'phral du dug ni kun min byed//de ltar 
61b6(phal chen): shes pa mi shes pa//shin tu sel ba byed par mdzod//sangs rgyas bla na gang yang med//de dang 'dra ba'ang yod pa min//de las gzhan du 'gyur ba med//de bas rgyal ba shin tu dkon//de nas byang chub 
61b7(phal chen): dpa' brtson 'grus dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa de dag smras pa//chos rnams 'di dag 'gal med par//
61b8(phal chen): mkhyen nas sangs rgyas thugs su chud//des ni mi mkhyen gang yang med//ye shes kyi yang pha rol phyin//ji ltar gser dang sa le sbram//ngo bo nyid kyis 'gal ba med//de ltar chos 
62a1(phal chen): @#//smin de bzhin chos//de dag thams cad mtshan nyid gcig/sems can sems can ma yin pas//ngo bo nyid kyis rang bzhin med//gdags su med las 'byung ba ltar//chos 
62a2(phal chen): rnams rang bzhin de bzhin no//ma 'ongs dus ni ji ltar bur//'das par brjod par bya ba ste//de la mtshan nyid yod pa min//de bzhin mtshan nyid med pa'o//'brel dang skye dang 'gog pa dang //
62a3(phal chen): rang bzhin dang ni rang bzhin dag/rnam pa tha dad gyur pa ltar//sangs rgyas rang bzhin de bzhin no//mya ngan 'das pa gzung med pa//gnyis kyi dus na brjod par byas//de la bye brag gang yang 
62a4(phal chen): med//de bzhin gzugs la rnam rtog pa'o//ji ltar ci zhig bgrang byas shing //bgrang bar byed ces brjod pa yang //de la tha dad gang yang med//de bzhin gzugs kyi rang bzhin no//ji ltar gcig 
62a5(phal chen): nas gcig gi bar//mang po'i tshul du bgrang ba yis//dngos po grangs med 'gyur ba yang //blo'i rnam pa tha dad bstan//'jig rten khams rnams la la dag/bsam gyis mi khyab tshig gyur kyang //
62a6(phal chen): nam mkha' 'jig par 'gyur ba med//rang 'byung ye shes de bzhin no//ji ltar 'jig rten phyogs bcu'i//nam mkha'i so sor brtags pa ltar//de bzhin sangs rgyas rnams mthong ste//sangs rgyas 
62a7(phal chen): rnams la rtog mi mnga'//de nas byang chub sems dpa' stobs dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smra pa//ji ltar dus rnams gsum po 
62a8(phal chen): yis//sems can khams rnams brjod par 'gyur//sems can de dag thams cad kyang //phung po lnga las shin tu brjod//de rnams las kyi phung chen yin//las kyi rtsa ba'ang sems yin te//sems ni sgyu ma bzhin du bshad//
62b1(phal chen): sgyu mar gyur pa'i 'gro 'di'o//gang na 'jig rten 'di 'byung zhing //yang na ldog par 'gyur ba yin//'jig rten 'jig rten mi byed de//gzhan gyi rgyus kyang byas pa min//'jig rten 'di na 'byung pa 'byung //'jig 
62b2(phal chen): rten 'dir yang ldog par 'gyur//'byung zhes brjod par mi bya ste//de ni 'jig rten shes pa yin//gang la 'jig rten snang ba zhes//'du shes rnam par brtags pa yi//'jig rten zhes bya gang zhig yin//
62b3(phal chen): snang ba zhes bya ci zhig yin//dus gsum dag tu rgyud pa yin//phung po 'di ni 'jig rten yin//de dag 'gog pa snang ba zhes//'gro ba dag tu 'du shes gyur//phung po zhes bya 'di ni ci//phung po'i rang bzhin 
62b4(phal chen): ji lta bu//phung po rnams ni mi 'jig phyir//dngos po med ces rab tu brjod//phung po 'di dag rnam brtogs don//de dag rang bzhin stong pa yin//mi 'jigs don ni stong pa ste//dngos po med ces bya 
62b5(phal chen): ba'o//'jig rten ci bzhin sangs rgyas te//sangs rgyas chos kyang de bzhin no//sangs rgyas chos dang sangs rgyas rnams//ngo bo nyid kyis rang bzhin med//ma nor yang dag ji bzhin pa'i//chos 
62b6(phal chen): nyid 'di ni rtogs gyur nas//kun gzigs thams cad mkhyen pa yi//sangs rgyas kun na rab tu snang //de nas byang chub sems dpa' rig pa dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur 
62b7(phal chen): rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smras pa//rang bzhin dngos po med pa yi//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad kyi//sa'i khams rnams ci bzhin de//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul 
62b8(phal chen): ngo mtshar che//de'i rang bzhin 'di yin la//brjod cing bsngags pa mtha' yas te//'das pa'i 'jig rten khams rnams su//sus kyang brjod pa ma yin no//gzhan du sems can las kyis na//
63a1(phal chen): @#//shin tu bstan par brjod pa ste//sems can rnams na ma gtogs par//las shes bya ba ci mi dmigs//de bzhin stong pa'i las kyi kyang //brjod pa rnams ni tshad med par//
63a2(phal chen): sems can kun gyi las su 'gyur//de la 'ongs pa gang yang med//ji ltar kha dog dbyibs kyi rnams//las kyi rgyud ni bsam mi khyab//snang yang de dag de na med//rnam par rig pa'i dngos nyid 
63a3(phal chen): 'dra//de bzhin sems can gtso bo yi//sku ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa//brjod pa rnams kyang tshad med par//zhing rnams kun na rab tu snang //skus ni rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas min//sangs rgyas 
63a4(phal chen): rnams la sku mi mnga'//dpa' bo chen po chos sku ste//chos kun rtogs par thugs su chud//gang gis rang 'byung rnams kyi sku//yang dag ci bzhin mthong ba ni//sangs rgyas chos ni rnam kun dang //
63a5(phal chen): chos rnams la yang rmongs pa med//'das par gyur pa'i chos rnams kun//mya ngan 'das par shes gyur na//de dag shin tu mi gnas pa'i//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mthong ba yin//yang dag sangs 
63a6(phal chen): rgyas sus mthong zhing //dran pa shin tu bsgom byas te//mtshan med tha dad med pa'i rnams//de dag chos kyi rgyal po'i sras//de nas byang chub sems dpa'i blo dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi 
63a7(phal chen): mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smras pa//ji ltar ri mo'i las rnams la//mtshon rnams sna tshogs kun 'du ba//kun kyang 'byung ba chen por mnyam//sems 
63a8(phal chen): kyis yongs su brtags pa yin//khams la tshon rtsi de dag med//tshon rtsi la yang khams med de//khams rnams ma gtogs gzhan na yang //tshon ces bya ba gang yang med//sems la ri mo de med de//
63b1(phal chen): ri mo la yang sems med de//sems de ma gtogs gzhan du yang //ri mo gang yang mi dmigs so//sems de rtag pa ma yin pa//bsam gyis mi khyab tshad med cing //phan tshun shes pa med pa yi//
63b2(phal chen): gzugs rnams thams cad ston par byed//ji ltar ri mo mkhan gyi rnams//sems ni gang gis ri mo de//bris pa'i sems ni mi shes pa//de bzhin chos kyi rang bzhin no//sems ni ri mo mkhan dang 'dra//
63b3(phal chen): sems ni phung po byed pa ste//'jig rten khams na ji snyed pa'i//'jig rten 'di dag sems kyis bris//sems dang 'dra bsangs rgyas te//sangs rgyas ji bzhin 'gro ba'o//sems dang sangs rgyas dag la 
63b4(phal chen): yang //ngo bo nyid kyis zad pa med//sems kyi rgyud rnams ci 'dra ba//de dag sangs rgyas rab tu mkhyen//de phyir sangs rgyas rang bzhin gyis//'gro ba sna tshogs gyur par snang //lus kyi rnams la 
63b5(phal chen): sems med de//sems la'ang lus rnams yod pa min//sangs rgyas mdzad pa'ang rab tu byed//de ltar spyod pa ngo mtshar che//gang zhig dus gsum thams cad kyi//rgyal ba thams 
63b6(phal chen): cad shes 'dod na//sangs rgyas thams cad sems kyi dngos//chos kyi dbyings su blta bar gyis//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur 
63b7(phal chen): rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smras pa//gzung du med la 'dzin ces bya//snang ba med la snang ba ste//mnyan du med par nyan pa med//sems med pa'i sems dang ldan//
63b8(phal chen): tshad med pa la tshad ma yin//gang zhig 'dzin par su 'dod pa//de ni tshad med tshad ma yin//nam yang 'dzin par mi 'gyur ro//brjod du med pa smra byed pa//bdag la shin du sla ba ste//de'i don kyang rdzogs mi byed//
64a1(phal chen): @#//srog chags rnams kyang tshim 'gyur//de bzhin mtha' yas brjod ldan pa'i//brjod pa gang zhig smra 'dod pa//bskal pa grangs med zad byas kyang //rang 'byung rnams kyis brjod mi zad//yid 
64a2(phal chen): bzhin nor bu dag pa ni//kha dog kun tu rab tu snang //de la kha dog yod pa min//sangs rgyas yon tan de bzhin no//ji ltar nam mkha'i dri med cing //gzugs med bstan du med yin yang //gzugs 
64a3(phal chen): rnams thams cad ston par byed//nam mkha' snang ba gang yang med//de bzhin thams cad gzigs pa'i rnams//yon tan kun dang ldan ston kyang //de ni sus kyang mthong ba med//sems la'ang de ni 
64a4(phal chen): mi dmigs so//sangs rgyas sgra ni grag mod kyi//de bzhin gshegs pa sgra ma yin//sgra ni ma gtogs gzhan du yang //sangs rgyas shes par mi rung ngo //ji ltar bdag gi mthong 
64a5(phal chen): tshul du//'di ni shes bya'i gnas ma yin//sangs rgyas byang chub blang du med//de la rnam par rtog pa med//sangs rgyas chos rnams gang dag kyang //su la'ang sangs rgyas gsung ba med//
64a6(phal chen): sems can bsam pa ji bzhin du//de la de 'dra ston pa mdzad////rab mtshe ma'i gnas su byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pas//tshigs su bcad pa bstan pa'i le'u zhes bya 
64a7(phal chen): pa ste/nyi shu lnga pa////
64a8(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////bam po drug pa////de nas de'i tshe byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/byang chub 
64b1(phal chen): sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin shin tu rnam par phye ba'i rnam par 'byed pa zhes bya ba la mnyam par bzhag par gyur to//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pa byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin
64b2(phal chen): rnam par bye ba'i rnam par 'byed pa de la mnyam par bzhag ma thag tu/de'i tshe phyogs bcu re rer yang /'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong phrag bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'das pa'i pha rol nas
64b3(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan yon tan dang ldan pa zhes bya ba//sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong phragabacu'i rdul shin tu phra bsnyed kyis zhal bstan pa mthong ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis de la 'di skad ces
64b4(phal chen): bka' stsal to//yon tan dang ldan pa de ltar khyod byang chub sems dpa'i tinge nge 'dzin shin du rnam par phye ba'i rnam pa 'byed pa la mnyam par bzhags pa legs so/legs so//rigs kyi bu khyod la ni de bzhin
64b5(phal chen): gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong prag bcu'i rdul shin tu phrab snyed thams cad kyang mtshan 'thun pa sha stag gis/'di ltar bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang/
64b6(phal chen): sngon gyi byin gyi rlabs dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa bas shin tu bstan pas na 'di ltar chos bstan pa dang sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /'jig
64b7(phal chen): rten gyi khams la 'jug par bya ba dang /sems can gyi khams rnam par sgom pa dang/chags pa med pa la 'jug par bya ba dang/bsgribs pa med pa'i spyod yul dang/kun nas rab
64b8(phal chen): tu sbyor ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa la rab tu bsgrub pa dang/chos thams cad khong du chud par bya ba dang /dbang po thams cad yongs su shes par bya ba dang /chos bstan pa thams cad
65a1(phal chen): @#//yongs su bzung bar bya ba dang/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bcu pa rtsom pa rab tu 'thob par bya ba'i phyir/byin gyis brlab pa mdzad kyis/rigs kyi bu khyod kyis sangs rgyas kyi
