75b8(phal chen): de ltar chos kyi dbyings la zhugs pa ni sems can gyi khams la yang 'jug ste/de dag yongs su smin par byed cing rnam par 'dul la sems can rnams la gnas skyed pa yang med do//chos rnams kun nas thob
76a1(phal chen): @#//par byed kyang /chos la gnas mi bskyed do//byang chub kyi sems la zhugs pa'i yid kyis 'tshang rgya bar bya ba la yang rnam par spyod de/'tshang rgya bar bya ba de la gnas mi bskyed do//tshig gi laM 
76a2(phal chen): yang brjod de/tshig gi lam la gnas par mi byed de/sems can gyi rgyud la yang 'jug ste/sems can gyi rgyud la gnas par mi byed do//ting nge 'dzin kyang rab tu shes shing de dag la yang mnyam par gzhog ste/
76a3(phal chen): ting nge 'dzin de dag la gnas kyi sems mi skyed do//sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med par 'gro zhing 'jug la/de dag du phyin te mthong zhing gnas kyang gnas kyi sems mi bskyed do//de dag nas 'gro yang chags 
76a4(phal chen): pa'i sems mi bskyed de/de dag sems 'di ltar bskyed do//kye sangs rgyas kyi chos de ltar gzung ba med/sangs rgyas kyi byang chub de ltar bsgribs pa med/chos dang 'dul ba de ltar legs par bstan//
76a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa de ltar legs par gnas/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa de ltar legs par 'dug/byang chub kyi sems de ltar legs par gnas /byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar pa de ltar shin 
76a6(phal chen): tu rtogs/byang chub sems dpa'i rang bzhin de ltar chags pa med/byang chub sems dpa'i tshul de ltar gnas pa med/byang chub semasapa'i lam de ltar shin tu sbyangs/byang chub sems dpa'i lung bstan de 
76a7(phal chen): ltar byin gyis rlabs kyang /byis pa so so'i skye bo 'di dag ni shes pa med pa/mthong ba med pa/dad pa med pa/mos pa med pa/mi mkhas pa'i gnas la spyod pa/bsam pa rmugs pa/'khor 
76a8(phal chen): ba'i rgyud shin tu 'byung ba la gzhol ba/sangs rgyas blta ba la log par zhugs/'dren pa'i sa las nyams pa/lam shes pa'i drung nas gar ba/kha lo sgyur ba mi 'tshol ba/stobs bcu la rim gro 
76b1(phal chen): med pa|byang chub sems dpa' la drin du mi gzo ba/dma' ba la gnas pa/dngos po med pas 'jigs pa/rigs pas spangs pa/tshul bzhin ma yin pa la zhugs pa/mnyam pa nyid las log par 
76b2(phal chen): ltung ba/g.yang sa chen por rab tu ltung ba/bdud kyi bsam pa la zhugs pa/sangs rgyas kyi bsam pa las ring du gyur pa/srid pa la mngon par chags pa/sha stag go/zhes de ltar byang chub sems dpa' 
76b3(phal chen): sems can gyi khams la 'jug cing snying rje chen po de bas kyang cher bskyed nas snying rje chen po rgya cher byas te/gnas med pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba yang dag par rtsags nas/'di snyam du sems bskyed do//gal 
76b4(phal chen): te sems can re re'i phyir yul re re dang /phyogs re re yang bskal pa brjod du med par sems can re re 'dul ba'i phyir 'dug la/sems can cig la ji lta ba de bzhin du sems can gyi khams thams cad kyi don 
76b5(phal chen): du 'dug kyang bdag gis de'i phyir mi dga' ba mi bskyed de/yul de dang phyogs de nas kyang bdag gud du g.yo bar mi bya'o//gud du 'gro bar mi bya'o//skra'i rtse mo tsam la chos kyi dbyings thams cad 'dus par 
76b6(phal chen): byas te|skra'i rtse mo'i tshul de lta bus skra'i rtse mor chud pa re re yang sems can gyi khams yongs su smin par bya ba dang 'dul ba'i phyir/bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med par gnas par bya'o//
76b7(phal chen): skra'i rtse mo gcig la ji lta ba de bzhin chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa thams cad la yang de ltar te/se gol gtogs pa tsam du yang gnas kyi 'du shes mi bskyed do//gnas par mi bya'o//gnas 
76b8(phal chen): kyi sems mi bskyed do//skra'i rtse mo tsam re re la yang lus gnas med pa dang /chos gnas med pa dang /dran pa gnas med pa dang /smon lam gnas med pa dang /ting nge 'dzin gnas med pa dang /
77a1(phal chen): @#//rtogs pa gnas med pa dang //spyod yul gnas med pa dang /sems can gyi khams 'dul ba gnas med pa dang /chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa gnas med pas 
77a2(phal chen): phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur thug pa'i bskal par byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ni byang chub sems dpa' 'di ltar sems bskyed do//bdag gis chos kyi dbyings thams cad 
77a3(phal chen): sgyu ma lta bur shes par bya'o//bdag gis sangs rgyas thams cad bltar med pa'i tshul du mi g.yor lta bur shes par bya'o/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa gnas med pa'i phyir/rmi lam lta bur shes par bya'o//bdag 
77a4(phal chen): gis de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi chos bstan pa grag cha lta bur shes par bya'o/bdag gis las kyi rnam par shes pa'i mthus las byung bas 'jig rten thams cad sprul pa lta bur shes par bya'o/dag gi spyod 
77a5(phal chen): pa'i mthu las byung bas lus ni yid kyi rgyu las byung bar shes par bya'o//bdag gas sna tshogs dang 'dres pa'i phyir 'gro ba thams cad ni sems lta bur shes par bya'o/rnam par 'gyur ba med pas bdag gis chos 
77a6(phal chen): thams cad ni yang dag pa'i mtha' dang mnyam par bstan par bya'o//bdag gis phyogs bcu sems bskyed pas rgyas par bkang ste/chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/nam mkha'i mthas gtugs pa byang chub sems 
77a7(phal chen): dpa'i 'dod pa la gnas par bya'o/bdag gi sems yang bar sgyur pa dang /dran pa gnas med pas/sangs rgyas kyi chos khong du chud cing mkhas pa sems kyi skad cig gcig gis thob par bya'o zhes/de ltar byang 
77a8(phal chen): chub sems dpa' de de dag dang sangs rgyas la bdag med pa'i tshul gyis yang dag par lta zhing /sems can chags pa med pa'i mthar phyin pa/tshig gi lam gyi sa la smin par byed pa'i tshe dga' ba mtha' yas pa 
77b1(phal chen): thob po sangs rgyas dang rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos la mos pa/tshad med pa yang yang dag par rab tu 'thob po//sems can thams cad yongs su bskyab pa'i don bdag gis skyo ba'i sems mi bskyed do//
77b2(phal chen): sems can gyi khams yongs su ma smin cing ma dul gyi bar du skyo ba'i smon lam mi bskyed do//bdag gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad las rnam par 'das par 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad du rgyu bar byas te/
77b3(phal chen): smon lam gang la sems can gyi sgra yod pa dang /sems can gyi las dang /sems can nye bar len pa dang /sems can rnam par gzhag pa dang /sems can gyis 'du shes dang ldan pa dang /sems can gyi glong dang /
77b4(phal chen): sems can gyi 'du byed dang /sems can gyi yul dang /sems can gyi 'jug pa dang /sems can kyi ldog pa yod pa de la bdag gis gnas par bya'o//sems can thams cad 'dul ba'i dbang du smon lam de 
77b5(phal chen): las g.yo bar mi bya ste/sems bskyed pa gcig gis kyang gnas kyi 'du shes skyed par mi bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/bdag gis gnas med pa dang /mi gnas par bya ste/bdag gi don yongs su spyad par 
77b6(phal chen): bya'o//yongs su rdzogs par bya'o zhes bya ba 'di ni/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bdun pa ste/gnas pa med pa zhes bya'o//
77b7(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/byang chub sems dpa' semasad pa' chen po rnams kyi byang cub sems dpa'i spyod pa thub par dka' zhes bya ba brgyad pa gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/'di la byang chub sems dpa'
77b8(phal chen): sems dpa' chen po dge ba'i rtsa ba thub par dka' ba/gdul bar dka' ba/rab tu gdul bar dka' ba/phyed par dka' ba/zil gyis ma non pa/bsam gyis mi khyab pa/kun tu mtha' yas pa mi 'phrog pa 
78a1(phal chen): @#//bdag nyid che ba/rgyud kyi tshul gcig tu gyur pas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi zham ring na/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa spyod pa'i tshe//sangs rgyas kyi chos la shin tu dad pa yin/yangs par dad pa 
78a2(phal chen): yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i byang chub la shin tu mos pa yin/byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam gyis rgyun mi 'chad pa yin/bskal pa thams cad du mi skyo ba'i sems dang ldan pa yin/sdug bsngal thams cad 
78a3(phal chen): kyi sgyid lug par mi 'gyur ba med pa yin/bdud thams cad kyis phyir brgal du med pa yin/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi shin tu byin gyis brlabs pa yin/byang chub semasadapa'i dka' thub thams cad kyis 
78a4(phal chen): rnam par sbyangs pa yin/byang chub sems/dpa'i brtul zhugs kyis tshad mi shes pa yin/theg pa chen po'i smon lam phyir mi ldog pa yin/de la byang chub sems dpa'i spyod ba bsgrub par dka' ba la gnas te/
78a5(phal chen): sems bskyed pa gcig gis skal pa grangs med par 'khor yang phyir mi ldog pa yin/byang chub sems pa'i smon lam chen po yang mi gcod do//de mthong ba tsam gyis kyang /sems can rnams bla na med pa 
78a6(phal chen): yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub las phyir mi ldog par byed do//thos pa dang bsnyen bkur bas kyang /sems can rnam bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i bya chub las phyir mi ldog bar byed de//sems 
78a7(phal chen): can thams cad sems can med par yang shes te/sems can gyi khams yongs su gtong ba ma yin no//'di ltar ste dper na mnyan pa ni pha rol tu 'gro ba/dbung med par rab tu chud pa'i lus kyi las dben pa dang 
78a8(phal chen): ldan pa//ngag gi las dben pa dang ldan pa/yid kyi las dben pa dang ldan ba/shes rab rtog pa'i chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin ming dang sgra med pa/zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin grangs med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo dang ldan 
78b1(phal chen): pa 'thob ste/snying rje chen po yang yongs shing tshad med par byed do//gal te de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin stong phrag grangs med pa sems kyi skad cig gcig gis thob par byed kyang /byang chub sems 
78b2(phal chen): dpa' sems ting nge 'dzin dang ldan pa de la de lta bu'i rnam par 'khrug pas dran pa rnam par nus kyi/de'i phyir sems 'di snyam du skye'o//de ltar bdag gis sems can de dag thams cad bla na med pa'i rnam pa rnam 
78b3(phal chen): par dag pa la rab tu bkod de/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid las phyir mi ldog par byas la/phung po ma lus pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings su yongs su mya ngan las bzla'o snyam mo//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di ni byang 
78b4(phal chen): chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa lnga pa ste/rmongs pa med pa zhes bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'i chen po rnams kyi byang chub
78b5(phal chen): sems dpa'i spyod pa drug pa lta ba bzang po zhes bya ba gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'dil byang chub sems dpa' semsdpa' chen po dmigs su med cing bstan pa mi dmigs pa'i lus kyi las dben pa dang 
78b6(phal chen): ngag gi las dben pa dang /yid kyi las dben pa thob yin/ma chags ma bcings pa'i lus kyi las dang /ma chags ma bcings pa 'i ngag gi las dang /ma chags ma gcings pa'i yid kyi las ma chags 
78b7(phal chen): ma bcings par bstan pa/gnas pa med pa/mi gnas pa med pa/sems ji lta ba bzhin du gnas pa/sems kyi rang bzhin tshad med pa'i rjes su song ba/chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin 'byam klas pa'i rjes 
78b8(phal chen): su song ba/mtshan nyid mi dmigs shing /mtshan nyid bstan du med pa zab pa gting dpag dka' ba/de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin gyi rjes su song ba/rnam par smin pa dang bral ba'i skyon med pa/
79a1(phal chen): @#//mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas pa/'gog pa dang mthun par gnas pa/mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings kyi spyod yul can/zhi ba'i rgyud rab tu thob pa/dngos po med pa'i mul gyis gzhir 
79a2(phal chen): gyur pa tshig gi lam chad pa/'gro ba thams cad las 'phags pa/gnas med pa'i spyod pa rab tu thob pa/byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa'i mtshan ma la mkhas pa/rtogs pa bcings pa med pa la gnas pa/
79a3(phal chen): de kho na nyid kyi chos gnas kyi rjes su zhugs pa/'jig rten thams cad kyis mi brjod pa/'jig rten las 'das pa'i gnas dang ldan pa thob pa yin te/de 'di snyam du sems so/kye sems can 'di dag thams 
79a4(phal chen): cad ni rang bzhin 'di lta bu ste/'di ltar sems can med pa'i rang bzhin no//kye chos 'di dag thams cad rang bzhin 'di lta bu ste 'di ltar 'dus byas pa med pa'i rang bzhin no/kye zhing 'di dag thams cad ni 'di 
79a5(phal chen): lta bu'i mtshan nyid de/'di ltar mtshan nyid med pa'o/kye dus 'di dag thams cad ni 'ad ltar byung ba ste/'di ltar tshig gi lam la gzhol zhing chos kun gyi rnam par tshig gi lam la shin tu gnas kyang chos thams 
79a6(phal chen): cad ni tshig gi lam la mi gnas pa'o zhes/de de ltar chos thams cad zab pa yang rab tu shes so//'jig rten thams cad dben pa la yang rab tu'ang ma yin/tshu rol tu 'gro ba'ang ma yin/chu klung gi gzhung 
79a7(phal chen): la gnas pa'ang ma yin/'gro ba dang 'ong ba rgyun mi 'chad pa'i phyir pha rol ma yin par sems can rnams kyang sgrol lo//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'khor bar 'gro ba yang ma yin/mya ngan 
79a8(phal chen): las 'das par 'gro ba yang ma yin/'khor ba'i rgyud la gnas pa yang ma yin te/tshu rol gyi mu nas sems can rnams bskyod de/pha rol gyi dogs bde ba/'jigs pa med pa/'tshe ba med pa/'khrug pa 
79b1(phal chen): med par rab tu bzhag kyang sems can gyi khams bgrang ba'i tshul gyis mngon par chags pa med do//sems can gcig las bskyod nas mang po la mngon par chags par mi byed/mang po las bskyod 
79b2(phal chen): nas sems can gcig la mngon par chags par yang mi byed/sems can gyi khams 'phel bar yang mi byed/bri bar yang mi byed/sems canagyi khams skye bar yang mi byed/'gog par yang mi byed 
79b3(phal chen): zad par yang mi byed/stsogs par yang mi byed/rnam par rtog par yang mi byed/rnam pa gnyis su mi byed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' ni sems can gyi khams la zhugs 
79b4(phal chen): shing khong du chud pa ste/chos kyi dbyings ji lta bar sems can gyi khams kyang de bzhin te/sems can gyi khams dang chos kyi dbyings de la gnyis su med do//gnyis su med pa'i chos la ni bri ba 'am 
79b5(phal chen): 'phel ba 'am/'gag pa yang med de/chos kyi rang bzhin ni rang bzhin gyis med par 'gyur ba 'am/len pa 'am/gnas pa 'am/mngon par chags pa 'am/gnyis su 'gyur ba med do//de ci'i phyir 
79b6(phal chen): zhe na/chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings bstan pa'i tshul du gnyis su med pa'i phyir ro//de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi dbyings rab tu dbye ba'i mkhas pa khong du chud pa yin te/mtshan nyid rnam par 
79b7(phal chen): dag pa'i lus dang /dngos po med pa yongs su shes pa dang /chos kyi mtshan nyid dang rang bzhin yongs su rtogs pas mtshan nyid med pa la gnas pa yin/de ni sems can rnam pa sna tshogs kyang shes 
79b8(phal chen): sems can gyi grangs kyang shes te/'jig rten gyi khams rnams la'ang mngon par chags pa med do//sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du yang snang ngo//sangs rgyas rnams la yang 'jug cing khong du chud 
80a1(phal chen): @#//par byed de/las la rnam par rtog par mi byed do//don kyang shin tu rtogs pa yin no//don gyi yi ge yang yongs su brjod do//chos 'dod chags dang bral ba'i mthar phyin pa thams cad kyang shes so/
80a2(phal chen): rab tu shes so/khong du chud do//byang chub sems dpa'i lam dang yang mi bral te/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa las kyang phyir mi ldog go/chos kyi dbyings su bzung ba las dbang sgyur bar zhugs pas zad 
80a3(phal chen): mi shes pa'i spyod pa dang ldan pa yin no//'di lta ste dper na/gtsub ma las byung ba'i me'i rgyu tshad med pas/me'i dgos pa las ma 'gags pa yin te/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang /'jig rten thams 
80a4(phal chen): cad na sems can gyi don las 'gag pa med par zhugs pa yin te/kun gyis mthar byas pa'ang ma yin/kun gyi mtha' med par byas pa'ang ma yin no//nye bar len pa dang bcas pa'ang ma yin/nye bar 
80a5(phal chen): len pa med pa'ang ma yin no//gnas pa'ang ma yin/mi gnas pa'ang ma yin/'jig rten gyi chos kyi rjes su song ba yang ma yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos la gzhol ba yang ma yin no//so so'i skye'o yang ma 
80a6(phal chen): yin/'bras bu la gnas pa'ang ma yin no//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' thub par dka' ba'i sems dang ldan pa/spyad pa la spyod de/de ni nyan thos kyi theg pa mi ston to/rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa mi ston te 
80a7(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi chos kyang ston pa med do//'jig rten dang 'jig rten gyi khams nye bar len pa med do/sems can dang sems can 'jig rten pa nye bar len pa med do/nyon mongs pa dang rnam par byang ba nye bar len 
80a8(phal chen): pa med do/de ci'i phyir zhe na/chos thams cad kun nas nyon mongs pa med par byang chub sems pas shes so//chos thams cad nye bar blangs pa med par byang chub kyi sems dpas yongs su shes so/chos thams 
80b1(phal chen): cad zhugs pa med par byang chub sems dpas yongs su shes so/chos thams cad ldog pa med par byang chub sem dpas yongs su shes so//de de ltar zhi zhing phra ba dang zab pa dang /bzang po'i chos la spyod 
80b2(phal chen): pa'i tshe/spyod do zhes bya bar mi dmigs so//spyad par bya'o zhes bya bar mi dmigs swo//khams kyi yul dang /skye mched kyi yul dang /nang gi snang ba dang /phye rol gyi snang ba dang /phyi nang gi snang ba dang /
80b3(phal chen): smon lam yongs su bsgrub pa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa rnams dang /chos thams cad du yang 'ang mi dmigs so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar chos kyi dbyings thams cad la ni/gang nyan thos kyi theg pa la zhugs 
80b4(phal chen): pa 'am/rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa la zhugs pa 'am/byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa la zhugs pa 'am /bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu zhugs pa 'am/so so'i skye bo'i khams la zhugs pa 'am/
80b5(phal chen): kun nas nyon mongs pa'i khams la zhugs pa 'am /rnam par byang ba'i khams la zhugs pa 'am/'khor ba'i khams la zhugs pa'am/mya ngan las 'das pa'i khams la zhugs pa'i chos gang yang med do//de yang 
80b6(phal chen): ci'i phyir zhe na/'di la chos gnyis shes bya ba gang yang med de mi gnyis pa yang med do//'di lta ste dper na/nam mkha' ni phyogs bcur ma rnyed/mi rnyed/rnyed par mi 'gyur yang nam mkhar bgrang ba'i tshul yod do//
80b7(phal chen): de bzhin du byang chub sems dpas kyang chos gang yang dmigs pa med de/chos thams cad mngon par 'tshang rgya'o//zhes grangs su bgrang ba yang yod de/de ni yang dag pa bden pa ste/log pa ma yin no//de ni bya ba dang 
80b8(phal chen): mi 'gal ba'i spyod pa yang ston to/smon lam yang mi gtong ngo //sems can gyi khams yongs su smin pa dag gdul ba yang med do//chos kyi 'khor lo yang skor ro/rgyu dang 'bras bu yang 'jig par mi byed do/chos 
81a1(phal chen): @#//nyid dang yang 'gal bar mi byed do/'das pa dang ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang /yang mnyam par 'gyur ba yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyang rgyun chad 
81a2(phal chen): par mi byed/chos kyi tshul dang yang 'gal bar mi byad/chos kyi rgyud kyang rab tu shes so//chos kyi spobs pa yang 'jig par mi byed/chos thos pas kyang rlom sems med de/chos kyi 'byung gnas kyang rab tu thob/chos 
81a3(phal chen): brjod pa yang rab tu byed/mi 'jigs pa la yang rab tu gnas/sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa yang mi gtong ngo//'jig rten gyi chu la yang 'gog par mi byed/'jig rten mnyam pa nyid kyang rab tu ston te/'jig rten gyi 
81a4(phal chen): spyod pa rnams la yang gnas pa med do//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' thub par dka' ba'i sems dang ldan pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa la spyod de/sems can gyi khams kyang /sems can dmyal ba dang /
81a5(phal chen): byol song gi skye gnas dang /gshin rje'i 'jig rten nas gyen du skyod de/de dag bskyed nas kyang yongs su smin par byed do/'dul bar byed de/rab tu gzhog par byed do//'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung 
81a6(phal chen): ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi lam gyi rgyud las kyang mi g.yo'o//'jig rten gnas pa drin du ma gzo pas yongs su gang ba dang /dgras yongs su gang ba dang kun nas nyon mongs pas yongs su gang ba dang

88a3(phal chen): mi non chos mnga' ba//thub mchog de dag spyod pa 'di yin ni//ldog pa med pa de dag rnams//mi dma' chos dang ldan pa thub par dka' ba dag/smon lam stobs kyi mthar phyin pa//spyod legs mnga'

88b3(phal chen): ye shes shin tu zab pa'i sar//rjes su zhugs shin blo gros shin tu brtan pa rnams//mi g.yo brtan zhing gnas pa dag/brtul shug sa bstan pa de dag spyod 'di'o//chos kyi dbyings la 'jug 
88b4(phal chen): pa yis//rjes su 'jug pa'i pha rol phyin par gyur pa dag/rnam par 'phrul la brtson pa rnams//chos kyi nyi ma de dag spyod 'di'o//mi mnyam mnyam pa'i blo mnga' rnams//mi gnyis yongs su

114b2(phal chen): khyim mngon par 'phags pa gcig nas cig tu rgyud pa'i pha rol tu phyin par bya ba/lha'i gzhal med khang bye ba brgya stong phrag bcu'i lha'i bus dad par btsun pa/lha'i bu btsun pa bye ba brgya stong phrag bcu sa lhun shin 
114b3(phal chen): tu gzung ba/dbang bskur ba'i lha'i bu bye ba brgya stong phrag bcusa lhun kun nas bzung ba/byang chub sems dpa'i sems bye ba brgya stong phrag bculags par sems shing spyod pa/byang chub sems dpa'i btsun 
114b4(phal chen): pa'i sems bskyed pa bye ba brgya stong phrag bca'i yid dag par byed pa/byang chub sems dba'i dbang po bye ba brgya stong phrag bcurab tu sim par byed pa/byang chub sams dpa' lhag pa'i bsam pa bye ba brgya stong 
114b5(phal chen): phrag bcu yongs su dag par byed pa/byang cub sems dpa'i mos pa bye ba brgya stong phrag bachu rnam par dag par byed pa/byang chub sems dpa'i las bye ba brgya stong phrag bco yang dag par sbyong ba/byang chub sems 
114b6(phal chen): dpa' skyes pa'i rabs bye ba brgya stong phrag bcudurab tu ston par byed pa/byang chub sems dpa'i chos kyi so bye ba brgya stong phrag bcurub tu snang bar byed pa/byang chub semasadapa'i sa bye brgya stong phrag bcu rab tu sgrub

115a1(phal chen): @#//bcus yang dag par skyes pa/chos bye ba brgya phrag bcus nye bar bstan pa/rnam par 'phrul pa bye ba brgya stong phrag bcu rjes su song ba/yon tan bye ba brgya stong phrag bcu shin tu 'phags pa bshams so////
115a2(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//bam po bcu pa//ji ltar gling bzhi pa chen po 'di'i dga' ldan gyi gnas su lha'i dbang po rab dga' ldan 
115a3(phal chen): gyis khang pa rin po che thams cad kyi rgyan can du de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdan bting bar gyur pa de bzhin du/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa chen po'i dga' yang dga' ldan gyi gnas dag tu lha'i 
115a4(phal chen): rgyal po rab dga' ldan gyi khang pa rin po che thams chad kyi rgyan can dag tu rgyan 'di lta bu dang /tshul 'di lta bu dang /dad pa 'di lta bu dang /mos pa 'di lta bu dang /dga' ba 'di lta bu dang /rab tu dga' ba 'di 
115a5(phal chen): lta bu dang /zhes 'di lta bu dang /rim gro 'di lta bu dang /spro ba 'di lta bu dang /nye bar sems dpa' 'di lta bus de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdon bting bar gyur to//de nas lha'i rgyal po rab dga' ldan gyi
115a6(phal chen): gdan bting nas/zhes can gyi rim gro dang bcas par dga' ldan gyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha'i bu bye ba brgya stong phrag bcu dang lhan cig du dong ste/bcom ldan 'das bsu nas me tog grangs med pa'i sprin gyi char 
115a7(phal chen): ni rab tu phab/spos yon tan bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i char ni rab tu phab/phreng ba'i yon tan sna tshogs dang ldan pa'i sprin ni rab tu kye/gdugs rnam pa sna tshogs kyi sprin gyi char ni rab tu phab/lha rdzas 
115a8(phal chen): kyi gos rab tu phra ba'i sprin rab tu skye/rin po che yid du 'ong ba mtha' yas pa thams cad kyi sprin gyi char ni rab du phab/sems shin tu dad pas lha rdzas kyi lha ba lhub dang /phyang 'phrul gyi sprin gyi char ni kun tu 
115b1(phal chen): phab/gdugs spos rnam pa du ma'i sprin gyi kun tu gdugs/lha rdzas kyi tsan dan gyi phye ma dang /a ga ru'i phye ma dang /spos dam pa thams cad kyi phye ma'i sprin gyi char ni rab tu phab/lha'i bu mtha' 
115b2(phal chen): yas pas kyang rang rang gi lus kyi sprin shin tu skye/dga' ldan gyi ris su togs pa'i lha'i bu bye ba brgya stong phrag bcu dang /de ma yin pa'i lha'i ris nas 'dus pa'i lha'i bu rnams kyis kyang sems shin 
115b3(phal chen): tu dad pas zhes rab tu bskyed nas shin tu btud te phyag 'tshal to//lha'i bu mo grangs med pa dag kyang dga' zhing mos pa'i sems kyis bcom ldan 'das la rab tu sems so//byang chub sems dpa' 
115b4(phal chen): dga' ldan gyi gnas na spyod pa grangs med pa rnams kyang bar snang la gnas te/sangs rgyas la mchod par brtson pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas las 'das pa'i mchod pas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa byed do//rim gro byed do 
115b5(phal chen): phyag 'tshal lo//de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi byin brlabs dang /sangs rgyas kyi sngon gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba rgyu mthun pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i cho 'phrul bsam gyis mi khyab pas rol mo dkrol ba dang /brdung 
115b6(phal chen): pa'i tshogs grangs med pa yang sgra 'byin par gyur to//lha'i bu dga' ldan gyi gnas na 'khod pa rnams dang /lha'i bu mo rnams kyang bcom ldan 'das la ni lta/de bzhin gshegs pa 'byung ba/shin 
115b7(phal chen): tu dkon par ni rjes su sems shing /yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ni rnam pa'i mchog thams cad dang ldan pa sgrib pa med pa'i chos can no zhes yang dag par lta zhing de logs su mngon par lta bar gyur to//
115b8(phal chen): dga' ldan gyi gnas na 'khor ba'i 'khor thams cad dang /lha bye ba brgya stong grangs med pa bcom ldan 'das bsu ba'i phyir rin po che thams cad kyi me tog gi phur ma thogs pa dang /spos thams cad kyi phur 
116a1(phal chen): @#//ma thogs thogs pa dang /rin po che thams cad kyi phur ma thogs thogs pa dang/rgyan thams cad kyi phur ma thog thogs pa dang /lha rdzas kyi tsan dan thams cad kyi phye ma'i phur
116a2(phal chen): ma thogs thogs pa dang/lha rdzas kyi a ga ru thams cad kyi phye ma phur ma thogs thogs pa dag/lha rdzas kyi rin po che thams cad kyi phye ma'i pur ma thogs thogs pa dang lha rdzas kyi spos kyi me tog
116a3(phal chen): thams cad kyi phye ma'i phung ma thogs thogs pa dang /lha rdzas kyi me tog ma 'da' ra ba'i phur ma thogs thogs pa rnams kyang dong ste/bcom ldan 'das la gtor to//mngon par gtor to/rab tu
116a4(phal chen): gtor to//lha'i bu dga' ldan gyi gnas su gtogs pa'i bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa rnams kyang bar snang la 'khod de/ye shes kyi spyod yul la gzhol ba'i sems kyis spos dang bdug pa'i sprin
116a5(phal chen): gyis bdug pas bar snang brgyan par byas so//sangs rgyas la dga' zhing gzhol ba'i sems kyi sa lha rdzas kyi me tog thams cad kyi sprin gyi char gyis rab tu brgyan par byas so//de bzhin gshegs
116a6(phal chen): pa la zhes cher bya ba'i sems kyis lha rdzas kyi gdugs sna tshogs kyi sprin gyis bar snang yog par byas te/rgyan par byas so//sangs d+yas la mchod par gzhol ba'i sems kyis lha rdzas phreng ba sna
116a7(phal chen): tshogs kyi sprin rab tu dgye bas bar snang brgyan pa byas so//sangs rgyas las thos par mos pa'i sems kyis gser gyi dra ba grangs med pa las dril bu sna tshogs kyi dra ba 'khrol zhing sgra 'byin pas
116a8(phal chen): g.yog par byas te bar snang rgyan pa byas so//de bzhin gsheg pa la yon gyi gnas dam pa yin bar mdzad pa'i sems kyis bla re grangs med pa las rin po che thams cad kyi se mo da'i sprin lding zhing /
116b1(phal chen): 'phrul pa'i rgyan gyis bar snang brgyan par byas so//sangs rgyas la dge bar 'dun pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas kyi gzhal med khang grangs med par lha rdzas kyi rol mo brdung ba dang /dkrol ba thams cad kyi 
116b2(phal chen): sgra yid du 'ong ba phyud pas par snang brgyan par byas so //sangs rgyas skye ba shin tu bar dogs par dogs pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas gyi na bza' kha dog sna tshogs pa'i sprin dang /na bza' mtshungs med 
116b3(phal chen): pa'i sprin gyi char 'bab pa'i rgyan gyi bar snang brgyan pa byas so//sangs rgyas la mos pa mtha' yas pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas ci cod pan grangs med pa'i sprin las cod pan gyi char chen po 'khor zhing 
116b4(phal chen): 'bab pa'i brgyan gyis bar snang brgyan byas so//sangs rgyas yid la bde ba skyes pa'i sems kyis lhas kyi rin po che kha dog sna tshogs grangs med pa'i sprin la phreng ba sna tshogs kyi char rgyun mi 'chad 
116b5(phal chen): par 'bab pa'i brgyan gyis bar dang brgyan pa byas so//lha'i bu bye ba brgya stong grangs med pa yang lha rdzas kyi me tog tha dad pa grangs med pa lag na thogs te/me tog zad mi shes pa'i mdzod rab tu 
116b6(phal chen): bkye nas/bcom ldan 'das la mngon par gtor to//mos pa'i spos zhib mo snyed cing sangs rgyas la dad par gnas pas lha rdzas kyi spos dang /gdugs pa kha dog sna tshogs grangs me dpal na thogs 
116b7(phal chen): te bcom ldan 'das la phul lo//rgyan rgyan po sprul pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas kyi tsan dan gyi phye ma kha dog sna tshogs grangs mad pa lag na thogs te/bcom ldan 'das la mdon par gtor to//dga' ba'i 
116b8(phal chen): sems kyis bcom ldan 'das kyi zham ring ru 'brang zhing rin po che'i gdugs kha dog sna tshogs grangs mes pa lag na thogs te/bco ma ldan 'das la mngon pa 'gebs so//sangs rgyas mtha' yas pa la mos 
117a1(phal chen): @#//ba'i sems rnam par 'phel bas/gos rin po che kha dog sna tshogs grangs med pa lag na thogs te/bcom ldan 'das gshegs pa'i lam yog par byas so/de bzhin gshegs 
117a2(phal chen): par mi gro bya bar mos pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas kyi rgyal mtshan rin po che sna tshogs kyi kha dog can lag na thogs shing /bcom ldan 'das kyi zham ring du 'dod do//sangs rgyas la dga' ba mtha' yas 
117a3(phal chen): pa rnam par 'phel ba'i sems kyis lha rdzas kyi lhab lhub dang /phyang phrul kha dog sna tshogs grangs me dpa'i yang grangs med pa lag na thos te/de bzhin gshegs pa la phul lo/dad pa mi

117a6(phal chen): yod pa tshad med pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas kyi rol mo brdung ba dang /dgrol ba kha dog sna tshogs grangs med pa lag na thogs te/yid du 'ong ba'i sgra dbyangs phyung nas/bcom ldan 'das dgyes par byed do//

117a8(phal chen): pa'i chos las byung ba/rjen pa'i dri med pa/chags pa'i med pa'i sems tshim par byed pa/chos kyi tshul grab mos mngon par grub pa/blo tshad med pa'i yangs pa las byung ba /
117b1(phal chen): dad pa'i rtsa brtan pas rnam par spel ba/dge ba'i rtsa ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa las byung ba/dge ba'i rtsa ba dang 'phrul 'grangs med pas mngon par bsgrubs pa/sangs rgyas kyi 
117b2(phal chen): mchod pa yid kyi rang bzhin las byung ba/mngon par 'du bya ba med pa'i chos kyi sgo'i rgyas btab pas/chos kyi dbyings thams cad kun nas yog par byas so/lha rdzas las 'das pa'i 
117b3(phal chen): pha rol tu phyin pa las byung ba'i me tog gi bla re sna tshogs kyis chos kyi dbyings kun nas khebs par byas so//lha rdzas kyi las 'das pa mos pa rnam par dag pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yul 
117b4(phal chen): rgyas par 'gengs pa'i na bza' sna tshogs mngon par dga' bas chos kyi dbyings brgyan par byas so//chags pa med pa'i chos kyi rang bzhin rdo rje la yang dag par zhugs pa'i sems kyis lha rdzas las 
117b5(phal chen): 'das pa'i mi skye pa'i chos las byung ba'i dril bu tshom bur gcad pa'i dra bas chos kyi dbyings brgyad par byas so//chos thams cad sgyu ma lta bur rtogs pa'i sems kyis lha rdzasa'adas pa'i spos thams cad

118a1(phal chen): @#//pas chos kyi dbyings brgyan pa byas so//mi gnas pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba blang ba med pa las byung bas/lha rdzas las 'das pa'i rin po che sna tshogs kyi pad mos kun nas yog pa'i 
118a2(phal chen): rgyan gyis chos kyi dbyings brgyan par byas so//lha rdzas las 'das pa'i spos thams cad kyi mchog gi sprin gyis chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par byas pa dang /me tog kha dog mtha' yas pa sna tshogs kyi 
118a3(phal chen): sprin dang na bza' kha dog rnam pa sna tshogs rnam pa tha dad pa thams cad kyi sprin dang /tsan dan gyi dri zhim po mtha' yas par 'pho ba thams cad kyi sprin dang lha rdzas las 'das pa'i rinapo che thams cad kyi gdugs

118a8(phal chen): dga' bar gyur ba'i rim gro ngoms mi shes pas/de bzhin gshegs pa la mngon par bstod pa/tshig rnam par dbye zhing brjod pas/byang chub sems dpa' de dag chos kyi dbyings kyi lus nam mkha'i khams rgyas 
118b1(phal chen): par byed pa byin gyis rlabs te/sangs rgyas 'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba thams cad kyis yongs su gzung ba'i yid kyis dbang pos mngon par bstod do//lha'i ris gzhan dang gzhan nas 'dus

118b8(phal chen): mtshams med pa/yid tshad med pa yongs su mgu bar mdzad pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rigs rgyun mi gcod pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa mtha' dang dbus med pa 'byung ba brnyes pa/
119a1(phal chen): @#//'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs su byung pa/khams dang mos pa bgrang du med pa thams cad spyod par mdzad pa/byang chub sems dpa' 
119a2(phal chen): thams cad ye shes mngon par sgrub pa la 'jug par mdzad pa/byang chub kyi sems dpa' thams cad kyi dbang po rab dga' bar mdzad pa/chos kyi sprin gyis nam mkha'i khams thams cad du yog par mdzad pa/
119a3(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings thams cad ma lus par de bzhin gshegs pas 'dul ba'i byin gyis rlob pa/sems can thams cad kyi rtog pa bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs su rgyas par mdzad pa/mi gnas pa'i ye shes

119a8(phal chen): can la rim gror mdzad pa/ji ltar bsnyas pa'i chos kyi sbyin pa mdzad pa/sems can ji ltar 'dul ba bzhin du 'dul ba mdzad pa/mos pa dang bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dag par mdzad pa/gzugs

119b3(phal chen): ye shes rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbus med pa la zhugs pa/yon tan gyi mchog ye shes snying pos byung bas chos kyi dbyings thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi nyi ma shar ba/'gro ba thams cad du sngon gyi smon lam zad pa mi 
119b4(phal chen): mnga' bar chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin la gnas pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa ma nyams pa brnyes pa/bdag gi dang yongs su gzung ba med pas gnas pa mi mnga' ba/'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos 
119b5(phal chen): thams cad 'byung zhing 'jig rten gyi chos thams cad kyis ma gos pa/'gro ba thams cad kyis mi shes pa'i rgyal mtshan yang dag par bcad pa/'jig rten thams cad kyi mi shes pa chen po yongs su gsal ba'i

132b2(phal chen): ste//'jig rten du ni ma byung yang //'jig rten dag tu skye bos mthong //sangs rgyas rnams la zhing mi gnas//rgyal ba dag la nyin mtshan med//skad gcig mi yin zla ba min//rgyal ba la ni lo mi gnas//'jig rten dag na 'di skad brjod//
132b3(phal chen): nyi ma che ge mo zhig du/rang byung byang chub rgyal bas thob//sangs rgyas nyi ma rjes mi 'brang //de la bskal pa yod ma yin//dus kyang ma lus med pa ste//dus gsum yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas//mgon po rnams ni mnga' bar 'gyur//
132b4(phal chen): ji ltar nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni//nams kyang mtshan dang mi gnas ste//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid do//bskal pa gcig dang dus gsum du//'dren par bzhugs pa ma yin te//dus gsum zhes kyang rab tu brjod//sangs rgyas rnams kyi 
132b5(phal chen): chos nyid ltos//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdul dang bral ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//yang dag sangs rgyas sku cig ste//'dren 
132b6(phal chen): pa thams cad don gcig kyang //tshad med gzhan dang gzhan dag du//phyogs bcu kun na rab tu snang //gang gis ma lus thams cad kyi//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun khyab pas//sems can gtso bo thub pa yin//spyod yul rnams ni ci 'dra ltos//
132b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos nyid ni//nang gi skus kyang snang ba min//de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi rlabs//phyi rol skus kyang mi snang ngo //dus rnams kun tu las rnams kyis//ji ltar las rnam stsags pa bzhin//de bzhin so sor phye 
132b8(phal chen): ba'i skus//rigs su byung bar rab du bstan//rgyal ba'i chos nyid ma gtogs par//sangs rgyas sku ni ci 'dra ba//grangs su bgrang bar mi nus te//bsam pas lta yang shes mi nus//ji ltar bdag cag sems kyi las//bsam gyis mi 
133a1(phal chen): @#//khyab mi 'byung ba//de bzhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//sems kyi 'phrin las mi mnga'o//ji ltar zhing rnams bsam yas pa//sa la rnam par dag par mdzes//de bzhin sangs rgyas pas mi khyab//'gro ba dag na 
133a2(phal chen): mdzes par snang //ji ltar las rnams 'jug pa ni//rkyen gyis rab tu 'byung 'gyur ba//de bzhin rgyu rnams 'thun pa las//sangs rgyas shin tu mthong bar 'gyur//yid bzhin nor bu'i rgyal po ni//ji lta yid bsam rdzogs byed pa//
133a3(phal chen): de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid kyang //rtag tu smon lam rdzogs par sgrub//mtha' dang dbus med 'jig rten 'dir//'jig rten mgon po 'dren par gyur//de'i smon lam de dag gis//phyogs bcu 'di dag rgyal bas gang //de nas byang chub 
133a4(phal chen): sems dpa' dri ma med pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnams par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ye shes dkyil 'khor sangs rgyas te//'jig rten chos rnams rnam par 
133a5(phal chen): spyod//'jig rten chos rnams ma sbyangs nas//sangs rgyas chos rnams rab tu brjod//des ste sems can mnyam sangs rgyas//sems can la la bsam byas na//rgyal ba de dag thams cad mthong //sangs rgyas la ni gshegs pa med//
133a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas la ni dmigs byas nas//sems kyi sangs rgyas dmigs pas na//sems skyed pa dang rgyal mnyam par//de bzhin de dag rgyal ba mthong //dkar po chos kyis nyer brtan cing //gang gis grags pa rnam sbyangs pa//de dag 
133a7(phal chen): thams cad sems bskyed la//shes rab ldan pa rab tu bzhugs//dmigs pa dag dang byin gyi rlabs//mi'i gtso bo ston pa mdzad//de'i phyir dmigs pa kun tu yang //sangs rgyas rgyal ba gshegs par brjod//'jig 
133a8(phal chen): rten khams rnams 'di dag ni//sangs rgyas sku rnams bdag gi med//sems la bdag gi med pa bstan//byang chub rtogs la bdag gi med//mi'i seng ge'i chos nyid dang //dpa' bo stobs la rab tu gnas//lus dang mtshan 
133b1(phal chen): nyid mi 'dra ste//snang ba'i sems dang mnyam pa'i sku//ji ltar 'jig rten de bzhin sku//ji bzhin skur yang rgyal ba ste//sku'i rang bzhin shes pa ni//sangs rgyas chags pa med ces bya//sangs rgyas kun tu gzigs pa ste//
133b2(phal chen): chos rnams thams cad rab tu gzigs//sangs rgyas chos dang byang chub rnams//gang la'ang dmigs pa med pa yin//sangs rgyas la ni gshegs pa med//sangs rgyas gang na'ang mi bzhugs ste//yang dag sangs rgyas log pa med//
133b3(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa len mi mnga'//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dpal gyi rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas thams 
133b4(phal chen): cad kun tu khyab//zhing gcig la ni mi gnas te//thams cad du ni sangs rgyas gshegs//zhing rnams kun tu rab tu snang //sangs rgyas sku ni thams cad du//byin gyi rlabs dag rab tu 'byung //'tshang rgya chos kyi 'khor lo dang //bsam pa bzhin du 
133b5(phal chen): mya ngan 'da'//sangs rgyas la ni su zhig sems//ci phyir sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//yang dag sangs rgyas su yis mthong //rgyal ba sus kyang mthong mi 'gyur//ji ltar chos rnams ches nyid dang //ji ltar sangs rgyas spyod yul du//
133b6(phal chen): gang dag chos rnams gcig tsam yang //de bzhin nyid la mi 'jug go/'di ni 'jig rten 'di rgyal zhes//'jig rten dag na rnam rtog yod//gang gis ngo bo nyid shes na//'jig rten med de rgyal ba med//'jig rten dad par bya ba'i phyir//
133b7(phal chen): mgon po dag ni mngon sum ston//de dag gi ni lta ba 'di//shin tu dmigs par mi rung de//gang gis chags pa'i rnam bzlog cing //chags pa med pa dag 'byed pa//chags pa thams cad rnam bzlog nas//chags med chos nyid rtogs par 'gyur//
133b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas zhes brjod gang yin dang //gang gi rnam 'phrul snang gyur pa'i//chos ni dus gsum thams cad du//rnam pa kun tu mi rnyed do//gang gis chos nyid 'di shes shing //gang gis 'di bzhin yid shes na//thams cad mkhyen cing gzigs 
134a1(phal chen): @#//kun pa'i//de bzhin gshegs pa myur du 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam 'phrul ba//'di dag thams cad tshig gis bstan//yang dag sangs rgyas tshig lam med//tshig gi lam gyis rab tu brjod//de nas byang chub sems 
134a2(phal chen): dpa' chos kyi rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//gang gis rnam par 'phrul snang ba'i//sangs rgyas mthong ba med pa bas//'jig 
134a3(phal chen): rten khams rnams thams cad ni//sdug bsngal phung po blangs kyang bla//'gro ba gang gis byang chub du//sems ni nam yang ma bskyed pa//de dag gnas 'di thos gyur na//sangs rgyas byang chub 'grub par 'gyur//gang gis bsam 
134a4(phal chen): pa gcig gis kyang //'di la mos byed mkhas pa ni//mi'i mchog du mi 'gyur zhes//des ni the tsom mi bya'o//de'i rnam par 'phrul pa ni//bskal pa bye ba'i bar du dkar//su zhig 'di la mos byed pa//de dag bla na med par 'gyur//
134a5(phal chen): brjod du med pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//skad cig skad cig bsnyan bkur kyang //'di'i tshul la ma gnas na//de dag bsnyen bkur byas ma yin//gang las sangs rgyas brjod 'gyur ba'i//chos 'di gang gis thos gyur pa//des ni mi bde 
134a6(phal chen): kun kyis kyang //sbyang ba rnams ni blang bar bya//ji tsam chos kyi dbyings rnams dang //dus gsum gyi ni mgon po yi//sangs rgyas rnams ni brten pa yi//ye shes chen po gcig thos nas//phyi ma'i skal pa bye ba rnams//zhing rnams 
134a7(phal chen): re rer zang byas shing //'di'i tshul ni brtson byed na//de la byang chub dkon pa med//sems can rnams ni sngon mtha'i//'khor bar mi rnams gnas gyur kyang //des ni sangs rgyas rab byung ba//chos nyid 'di ni shes ma gyur//chos 
134a8(phal chen): rnams zhig pa byas pa med//chos ni 'jig par mi 'gyur te//kun tu snang las rnam 'phrul ba//'jig rten dag du thams cad bstan//dga' ldan gyi gnas su byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pa'i tshigs su bcad pa bstan pa'i le'u ste/nyi shu dgu pa'o////
134b1(phal chen): //sangs rgyas rmad gcad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////
135a1(phal chen): @#//par bya ba dang /sngon gyi mtha'i mur thug pa'i ye shes dang /dran pa rgyun mi chod par rnam par dag par bya ba dang /dbang po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byin kyi rlabs kyis/byin du rlabs par bya ba dang /chos kyi sgo mtha' 
135a2(phal chen): yas pa bstan par bya ba dang /shes pa kun tu gzung bar bya ba dang /'di lta ste yongs su sngo ba bcu yongs su rdzogs par bstan pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba gzung bar bya ba dang /'jig rten las 'das 
135a3(phal chen): pa'i tshogs rnams rab tu bstan par bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes mi chod par bya ba dang /smon lam chen po'i skabs dbye bar bya ba dang /don shin tu bstan pa la 'jug par bya ba dang /chos kyis dbyings yongs su shes 
135a4(phal chen): par bya ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i thams cad kyi yid du rab tu dga' bar bya ba dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi srid pa gsum gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba 'thun pa la brten par bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs yang 
135a5(phal chen): dag par gzung bar bya ba'i phyir byin kyis brlab pa mdzad gyis//kye rigs kyi bu khyod kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i mthu dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin kyis rlabs dang /'jig rten las 'das pa'i bsod nams kyis brtan pa dang /
135a6(phal chen): gzungs kyi snang ba la zhugs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos bsgribs pa med pa la zhugs pa dang /chos kyi dbyings snang ba'i chos nyid du bya ba dang /kha na mtho ba med pa'i chos dkar po yang dag par bsgrub par bya ba dang /ye shes kyi spyod 
135a7(phal chen): yul yangs pa la gnas pa dang /bsgribs pa med pa'i chos snang ba la gnas pas chos bstan pa 'di brjod pa dang /bstan pa'i spobs par gyis shig/de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis/byang chub sems 
135a8(phal chen): dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyi ye shes tshad med pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do//spobs pa chags pa med pa dang /tshig rab tu dbye ba la mkhas pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad/bsgribs pa med pa'i chosakyi snang ba nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad de//
135b1(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sku dang /gcig pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do//tshad med pa'i dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag par nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad de//byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin bsam gyis mi khyab pa rnam par dbye ba 
135b2(phal chen): la mkhas pa nye bar bsgrub par mdzad do//dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad nyams pa med par yongs su bsngo ba'i ye shes nye bar bsgrub pa mdzad do //chos thams cad kyi lhag mthong mngon par bskyed pa la mkhas pa nye bar bsgrub ba mdzad do//
135b3(phal chen): chos thams cad bstan pa'i gnas rgyun mi chad pa nye bar bsgrub pa mdzadado//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di lta ste/ting nge 'dzin 'di nyid kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i gzi brjid kyi phyir ro//de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gi 
135b4(phal chen): phyag g.yas pas/byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyi sbyo bo la byug pa mdzad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasade dag gis byang chub sems dpa' rdo rjergyal mtshan la reg ma thag tu ting nge 'dzin de las rnam par langs te 'das 
135b5(phal chen): nas kyang byang chub sems dpa' de dag la smras pa/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam 'di lta ste/'gro ba thams cad khyab par byed pa/sems can thams cad yongs su skyob pa 'di ni bsam gyis mi khyab ste//
135b6(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/de la gnas shing byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams/'das pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'ad sa rnams kyi yongs su bsngo ba slob po/ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das rnams kyi 
135b7(phal chen): yongs su bsngo ba la slob par 'gyur ro//da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'a das rnams kyi yongs su bsngob la slob po/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po rnams kyi yongs su bsngo gang la bsams 
135b8(phal chen): te 'di skad du/smras pa de dag ni gang /kye rgyal ba'i sras dgayongs su bsngo ba'i grangs du zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/bsngo ba bcu po 'di dag ni 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams 
136a1(phal chen): @#//kyis gsungs so//gsung ngo //gsung bar 'gyur ro//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sems can rnams kyi 'du shes 'grog cing 'gro ba thams cad yongs su skyob pa zhes bya ba byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yongs su bsngo ba dang po dang /
136a2(phal chen): mi phyed pa dang ldan pa zhes bya ba byang chub se mas dpa' rnams gyi yongs su bsngo ba gnyis pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad la yang dag par bskyed pa zhes bya ba byang chub kyi sems dpa' rnams kyi yong su bsngo ba gsum pa dang /thams cad kyi rjes su song 
136a3(phal chen): ba zhes bya ba byang chub sem dpa' rnams kyi yongs su sngo ba bzhi ba dang /bsod nams kyi mdzod mi zad pa zhes bya ba byang chub ses dpa' rnams kyi yongs subasngo ba lnga pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid kyi rjes su song ba zhes bya ba 
136a4(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi yongs su bsngo ba drug pa dang /'gro ba thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi rjes susong ba zhes bya ba byang chub semasadapa' rnams kyi yongs su pasngo ba bdun pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin zhes bya ba byang 
136a5(phal chen): chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yonas su bsngo ba brgyad pa dang /chags pa med cing bcings pa dang dkrol ba med pa zhes bya ba byang chubasems dpa' rnams kyi yongs sa bsngo ba dgu pa dang /chos kyi dbyings tshad med pa'i rjes su song ba zhes bya byang chub 
136a6(phal chen): sems dpa' rnams kyi yong su bsngo ba bcu pa ste//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yangs su bsngo ba bcu po de dag ni/'das ba dang ma byon pa dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis gsungs so//
136a7(phal chen): gsung ngo //gsung bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi yongs su bsngo ba dang po sems can rnams kyi 'du shes 'gog cing 'gro ba thams cad yongs su skyob pa zhes bya ba gang zhe na//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag 'di la byang 
136a8(phal chen): chub sems dpa' sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la rab tu sbyor ba 'am/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rnam par sbyong ba 'am/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa so so ma dpa' 'am/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la brtson pa'am/bsam gtan 
136b1(phal chen): gyi pha rol tu phyin pa la rnam par spyod pa 'am/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la 'jug pa 'am/byams pa bsgom pa yang dag par skyed pa 'am/snying rjes snying rtse bar byed pa 'am/dga' ba la rab tu dga' bar byed pa//btang snyoms la shin tu 
136b2(phal chen): gnas pa byed kyang rung ste/dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams la shin tu bsams nas/dge ba'i rtsa ba de dang de dag la rab tu sbyor zhing /dge ba'i rtsa ba tshad med pa rnam par phye nas//'di ltar dge ba'i rtsa ba gang gis sems can rnams kyi sems can dmyal ba'i ngan song 
136b3(phal chen): rgyun chad par 'gyur ba dang /byol song gi skye gnas pa dang /gshin rje'i 'jig rten pa rnams kyi sdug bsngal gyi phung po rnam par ldog par 'gyur ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba 'gro ba thams cad la rnam par dag pa'i mthar phyin par phan dgos pa gang yin 
136b4(phal chen): zhes sems skyed do//de de ltar dge ba'i rtsa ba de dag yang dag par skyed pa na/'di ltar yongs su bsngo ste/dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dis sdug bsngal gyi phung po thams cad rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir bdag sems can thams cad kyi gnas su gyur cig nyon mongs 
136b5(phal chen): pa thams cad las yongs su dgrol ba'i phyir/bdag sems can thams can kyi bung gnyen du gyur cig/'jigs pa kun las bsrung ba'i phyir bdag sems can thabs cad kyi skyabs su gyur cig/thams cad mkhyen pa'i sa thob par bya ba'i phyir/
136b6(phal chen): bdag sems can thams cadakyi lam du gyur cig/shin tu bde ba dang ldan pa thob par bya ba'i phyir/bdag sems cad thams cad kyi rten du gyur cig/rab rib med pa'i ye shes snang ba'i yang dag par bstan pa'i phyir/bdag sems can
136b7(phal chen): thams cad kyi snang bar gyur cig/ma rig pa'i mun pa rnam par bsal ba'i phyir/bdag sems chan thams cad kyi sgron mar gyur cig/shin tu dag pa la rab tu dgod pa'i phyir/bdag sems chan mtha' cad kyi mar mer gyur cig/chos kyi 
136b8(phal chen): tshul la gzung ba'i phyir/bdag sems la thams cad kyi 'dren par gyur cig/bsgribs pa med pa'i ye shes kyi phung po bstabs pa'i phyir/bdag sems cang thams cad kyi yongs su 'dren par gyur cig ces/de de ltar 'gro ba thams cad
137a1(phal chen): @#//kyi don du thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi mthar shin tu thug par dag pa'i rtsa ba de dag yongs su bsngo ste/ji ltar mdzad bsrung ba'i phyir yongs su bsngo ba de ltar/mi mdza' ba bsrung ba'i phyir yang yongs su bsngo'o//de ci'i 
137a2(phal chen): phyir zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams la bdag tu bya ba'i bsam pas mi mdza' ba ni gang yang med de/de yang 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' ni chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid la yang dag par byung ba ste/de 'di snyam du 
137a3(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' la mi mdza' ba'i don byed pa de dag thams cad ni 'god pa'i rgyud la 'god 'god pa yin no zhes te/de dag ni sems can thams cad kyi yul thams cad la zhe sdang ba med pa'i mig gis lta'o//byang chub sems dpa' 
137a4(phal chen): sems dpa' chen po rnams ni 'gro ba thams cad kyi dge ba'i bshes nyen te/sems can thams cad la dge ba'i chos yang dag par bstan pa'i phyir 'jug go/'di lta ste dper na/rgya mtsho chen po ni dug thams cad kyis gdugs 
137a5(phal chen): par bya ba mi nus so//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang drin mi gzo pa dang /blo zhan pa dang /sems 'khrug pa dang /rnyogs pa dang /rmun par gyur pa dang /rang bzhin bstor pa dang /rlom sems dang ldan pa dang /sgrub pa nyams 
137a6(phal chen): pa dang /sems rmus long lta bur gyur pa'i sems can byis pa thams cad kyis skyon can du mi nus so//'di lta ste dper na/lha'i bu nyi ma 'char ba de ni rmus long gi skyon gyis 'char ba las thogs par mi 'gyur ro//dri za'i grong khyer gyi 
137a7(phal chen): skyon gyis thogs par mi 'gyur ro|/gling bzhi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi sa'i rdul gyi skyon gyis thogs par mi 'gyu ro//lha ma yin gyi dbang po sgra gcan gyi skyon kyis thogs par mi 'gyur ro////du ba 'gribs pa'i skyon kyis thogs par mi 
137a8(phal chen): 'gyur ro//'dzam bu'i gling ki nye ba'i nyon mongs pa'i skyon gyis thogs par mi gyur ro//grib ma sna tshogs kyi skyon gyis thogs par mi 'byung ngo//dug gi ri'i skyon gyis thogs par mi 'gyar ro//khams kyi nye ba'i nyon mongs pa'i skyo gyis 
137b1(phal chen): thogs par mi 'gyur ro//dus yongs su 'gyur ba'i skyon gyis thogs par mi 'gyur ro/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po dran pa can yang dag par shes pa dang ldan pa/sems kyi bdag nyid che ba nyid shin tu 
137b2(phal chen): zab cing yangs pa yid la yang dag par rtog pa/mi zhan pa yon tan spyad pas ye shes mthar gyur pa/chos la shin tu dad pa'i sems mngon par skyes pa/chos kyi snang ba'i 'od du rab tu snang ba/don thams cad mthong pa/
137b3(phal chen): chos mngon par shes pa/ye shes rig pa rtsod pa med pa lhur byed pa/sems can thams cad dge ba'i chos spyod pa la rab tu sbyor ba/sems can thams cad kyis nyes pa'i sems bskyed pa la 'khrul pa med pa de yang dge ba'i rtsa ba de 
137b4(phal chen): dag yongs su bsngo ba ni/gnod par byed pa thams cad kyi skyon gyis thogs par mi 'byung ro//sems can mi srun ba'i tshul gyis ldog par mi 'gyur ro//sems can gyi lta ba'i rnyog pas dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su sngo ba dang /bral 
137b5(phal chen): bar mi 'gyur ro//sems cad kyi sems 'khrugs pas ldog par mi 'gyur ro//sems can gyi sems kyi rgyud stor bas byang chub sems dpa'i go cha chen po bgos pa las ring bar mi 'gyur ro//'gro ba thams cad yongs su bskyab 
137b6(phal chen): pa'i smon lam dkrugs ma'i rnyog pa thams cad kyis bcil bar mi 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa' rtsom pa chen po sems can drin mi gzo pa mthong bas lhod par mi 'gyur ro//byang chubatud migs pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba sems

169b2(phal chen): te//mi'i dam pas shin tu gang gyur cig/de dag de 'dra'i yongs sngo sems ldan pa//gang gis ma lus 'jig rten byang chub du//dus gsum rgyal bas mngon par sangs rgyas pa//de dang ldan par yongs su sngo ba

169b4(phal chen): yangs pa yi//yongs su bsngo ba chags pa med cing //bsod nams zad med de dag mdzod du zhugs//de ni dus gsum kun tu zad mi 'gyur//de dag spyod pas chos la 'jal bar byed//de dag byed pa med par chos

191b5(phal chen): rgyas kyi ye shes dang yon tan dgongs pa dam pa rnam par dag par bya ba'i phyir yongs su sngo'o//sems rin po che mtha' yas pa la dbang sgyur ba la rab tu 'thob par bya ba'i phyir yongs

271a5(phal chen): rang bzhin mi ldan ji ltar de bzhin nyid//de ltar las rnams mkhas pa yongs su sngo//de bzhin gnyid kyi mtshan nyid ci 'dra dang //de bzhin nyid kyi ngang tshul ci 'dra dang //
271a6(phal chen): de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhinaci 'dra ba//de 'drar las rnams kun kyang yongs su sngo//de dag mtha' yas las la rab zhugs shing //de bzhin nyid de ji ltar mtha' yas te//
271a7(phal chen): de la chags dang bcings pa med pa ltar//de ltar las rnams shin tu dag par byed//de ltar rgyal sras shin tu mkhas pa dag/bsam pas mi g.yo shin tu brtan thob pa//
271a8(phal chen): ye shes stobs ldan tshul la mkhas gyur pa//de dag rgyal ba'i mdzod kyi tshul la zhugs//de dag tshul gyi rgyal po'i tshul shes pas//de la chags dang bcings ba med pa'i 
271b1(phal chen): phyir||chags med sems ni 'jug par mi 'gyur te//'jug par byed pa'i dbang yang dmigs pa med//chos kyi lus ni rab tu thob byas shing//'gro ba kun 
271b2(phal chen): las mtshan nyid ji bzhin pa//chos nyid de la mtshan nyid med gsungs pa//de 'dra'i mtshan nyid chos la rab tu zhugs//de ltar de dag 'grogs pa bsam mi khyab//
271b3(phal chen): de la sems ni lhag ma med par byed//bsam yas chos rnams kun la rab tu zhugs//sems ni sems min rnam par dben par shes//de dag chos dben nyid la 'jug 
271b4(phal chen): byed cing//de ltar chos nyid rjes su sems pa byed//g.yo dang rlom sems thams cad 'gags pa ste//de phyir yon tan rnam par grags pa yin//de dag rnam smin 
271b5(phal chen): thams cad ma lus par//ye shes rgyas btab zad par mi 'gyur te//de ni mi zad mi zad nyid kyi dngos//mi zad mtha'i tshul dang 'gal ba med//bsam pa lus kyi 
271b6(phal chen): phyi rol yod pa min//sems ni nang na yang yod pa ma yin te//dus rnams gang na yang sems ni yod pa min//rlom sems rlom chos med pas zhi bar 'gyur//de 'dra'i smon 
271b7(phal chen): lam ldan pa rgyal sras rnams//chos kyi rang bzhin zhi ba stong pa ste//chos rnams ma lus thams cad g.yo ba med//bdag nyid rgyal bas rnam par sangs rgyas med//
271b8(phal chen): de bzhin nyid gang 'gro ba kun mnyam pa'i//chos nyid mtshan nyid rjes su shes byas shing//bsam yas mtshan nyid de dag mthong gyur nas//mtshan nyid med pa'i shes la rmongs pa med//
272a1(phal chen): @#//de ltar zab pa'i spyod la rab brtson pa//de dag byang chub brtag par brtson pa ste//de dag smon lam de yang bzlog pa med//'gro ba kun gyi don tu de dag brtson//de dag 'jig rten 
272a2(phal chen): rnams las 'das gyur cing//'chi 'pho yongs su rtog pa med byas te//sprul pa 'dra bar sems kyang rig byas nas//de dag 'gro ba gdul phyir rab du 'jug/de dag mthong dang dran pa 
272a3(phal chen): 'di 'dra ste//las kyi smin pas 'jig rten byung ba yi//khams gsum pa'i sems can thams cad la//de dag yongs su bskyab par mngon par brtson//'gro ba kun gyi 'du shes rgyu 
272a4(phal chen): ba dang //rnam par rtog pa ji snyed tha dad pa'i//bskal pa mi mnyam de dag de bzhin shes//ngo bo nyid las nam yang phyir mi ldog/shes pas mkhas pa chos rab bsngags pa'i rnams//
272a5(phal chen): 'gro ba kun la snying rtse'i sems ldan pas//chos kyi tshul la rjes su sems byed cing //tshul mkhas de dag de ltar yongs su bsngo'o//de bzhin nyid kyi mtshan nyid kyi rang bzhin zhes bya ba ste/yongs su bsngo ba brgyad pa'o//
